Perfect! I now have comprehensive understanding of the codebase. Let me compile my findings into a complete report.

## Summary: Database Schema, Message Storage, and Chat UI Architecture

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's what I found:

---

### 1. Message Table Schema (SurrealDB)

**Location:** `/schema/surreal-schema.surql` (lines 15-24)

The `message` table is SCHEMAFULL with these fields:

```surql
DEFINE TABLE message SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD conversation ON message TYPE record<conversation>;
DEFINE FIELD role ON message TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ["user", "assistant"];
DEFINE FIELD text ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD createdAt ON message TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD suggestions ON message TYPE option<array<string>>;
DEFINE FIELD clientMessageId ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON message TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE INDEX message_conversation_createdAt ON message FIELDS conversation, createdAt;
DEFINE INDEX idx_message_embedding ON message FIELDS embedding HNSW DIMENSION 1536 DIST COSINE;
```

**Key observations:**
- Messages have only `role`, `text`, `conversation`, and optional `suggestions`
- **No tool_call field exists on messages** - tool calls are NOT persisted to the database
- Embeddings are stored separately for vector search
- Suggestions field stores an array of strings (not tool results)

---

### 2. Tool Call Handling (Not Persisted)

**Tool calls are ephemeral** - they exist only during streaming and are not saved to the database.

**How they flow:**

1. **Chat Agent Execution** (`handler.ts`, lines 117-120):
   ```typescript
   if (part.type === "tool-call") {
     logInfo("chat.agent.tool_call", "Chat agent invoked tool", part);
     toolCalls.push({ name: part.toolName, args: (part as any).input });
   }
   ```
   Tool calls are collected during streaming but only used for side effects (entity extraction, observations, etc.)

2. **Chat Route** (`chat-route.ts`, lines 223-272):
   - Uses Vercel's AI SDK `streamText()` which handles tool execution
   - Uses `.toUIMessageStreamResponse()` to stream the response
   - Tool calls result in entities being written to the graph, not stored as tool call records

3. **Tool Results** - Entities extracted from tool calls are persisted separately:
   - Extraction relations (`extraction_relation` table) - tracks what was extracted from what source
   - Entity records (`task`, `decision`, `question`, `project`, etc.)
   - These links provide provenance, not the tool call itself

---

### 3. Conversation/Message Relationship Structure

**Database Schema** (`surreal-schema.surql`):

```surql
DEFINE TABLE conversation SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD workspace ON conversation TYPE record<workspace>;
DEFINE FIELD messages ON conversation TYPE option<array<record<message>>>;
DEFINE FIELD discusses ON conversation TYPE option<record<project | person | feature | task | decision | question | observation | suggestion>>;
```

**Relationship Flow:**
- One conversation has many messages (via `messages` array field)
- Conversation optionally "discusses" one entity
- Messages are ordered by `createdAt`
- Messages link back to conversation via `conversation` field

**Extraction Relations** - Connect messages to entities:
```surql
DEFINE TABLE extraction_relation TYPE RELATION IN message | document_chunk | git_commit 
  OUT workspace | project | person | feature | task | decision | question SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD confidence ON extraction_relation TYPE float;
DEFINE FIELD extracted_at ON extraction_relation TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD resolved_from ON extraction_relation TYPE option<record<message>>;
```

This allows querying: "What entities were extracted from this message?"

---

### 4. Frontend Message Rendering

**Location:** `/app/src/client/routes/chat-page.tsx` (lines 100-146)

Messages render parts that can include text, reasoning, and tool invocations:

```typescript
message.parts.map((part, i) => {
  if (part.type === "text") {
    // Markdown text with entity links
  }
  if (part.type === "reasoning") {
    // Collapsible <details> block for extended thinking
    <details key={i} className="thinking-block">
      <summary>Thinking</summary>
      <pre className="thinking-content">{part.text}</pre>
    </details>
  }
  if (isToolPart(part)) {
    // Tool invocation display
    const toolPart = part as { type: string; state: string; output?: unknown };
    const toolName = toolPart.type.replace(/^tool-/, "");
    
    if (toolName === "create_suggestion" && toolPart.state === "output-available") {
      return <SuggestionToolCard key={i} output={output} />;
    }
    return (
      <div className="chat-tool-invocation">
        <span className="chat-tool-name">{toolName}</span>
        {toolPart.state === "output-available" ? (
          <span className="chat-tool-status">Done</span>
        ) : (
          <span className="chat-tool-status--running">Running...</span>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
})
```

**UIMessage Structure** (from Vercel AI SDK):
- `id`: Message identifier
- `role`: "user" | "assistant"
- `parts`: Array of message parts
  - Text parts: `{ type: "text"; text: string }`
  - Reasoning parts: `{ type: "reasoning"; text: string }`
  - Tool parts: `{ type: "tool-<name>"; state: "pending" | "output-available"; output?: unknown }`
- `metadata`: Custom metadata (conversationId, onboardingState)

---

### 5. Tool Execution UI Patterns

**Existing Collapsible/Expandable Patterns:**

1. **Reasoning Block** (lines 117-123 of chat-page.tsx):
   ```typescript
   <details className="thinking-block">
     <summary>Thinking</summary>
     <pre className="thinking-content">{part.text}</pre>
   </details>
   ```
   This is an expandable `<details>` element showing extended reasoning.

2. **Suggestion Tool Card** (SuggestionToolCard.tsx):
   Special handling for `create_suggestion` tool output:
   ```typescript
   <div className="suggestion-tool-card">
     <div className="suggestion-tool-card-header">
       <span className="suggestion-tool-card-category">{output.category}</span>
       <span className="suggestion-tool-card-confidence">{Math.round(output.confidence * 100)}%</span>
     </div>
     <div className="suggestion-tool-card-text">{output.text}</div>
     <div className="suggestion-tool-card-rationale">{output.rationale}</div>
   </div>
   ```

3. **Generic Tool Invocation** (lines 134-143):
   Shows tool name and state (Running/Done) - minimal display for other tools.

---

### 6. SSE Event Types (Server → Client Streaming)

**Location:** `/app/src/shared/contracts.ts` (lines 171-236)

The streaming pipeline uses these event types:

```typescript
type StreamEvent =
  | TokenEvent              // Individual text tokens
  | ReasoningEvent          // Extended reasoning tokens
  | AssistantMessageEvent   // Final assistant message text
  | ExtractionEvent         // Extracted entities/relationships
  | OnboardingSeedEvent     // Onboarding initial seeds
  | OnboardingStateEvent    // Onboarding state transitions
  | ObservationEvent        // Observations created/updated
  | DoneEvent               // Stream completion
  | ErrorEvent;             // Stream error
```

**Event Details:**

```typescript
type TokenEvent = { type: "token"; messageId: string; token: string };
type ReasoningEvent = { type: "reasoning"; messageId: string; token: string };
type AssistantMessageEvent = { 
  type: "assistant_message"; 
  messageId: string; 
  text: string; 
  suggestions?: string[]; 
};
type ExtractionEvent = {
  type: "extraction";
  messageId: string;
  entities: ExtractedEntity[];
  relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
};
type ObservationEvent = {
  type: "observation";
  messageId: string;
  action: "created" | "acknowledged" | "resolved";
  observation: ObservationSummary;
};
type DoneEvent = { type: "done"; messageId: string };
type ErrorEvent = { type: "error"; messageId: string; error: string };
```

**Flow in chat-processor.ts** (lines 248-273):
1. Extraction events emit extracted entities/relationships
2. Assistant message event with final text and optional suggestions
3. Done event signals completion
4. Error event if processing fails

---

### 7. Message Persistence

**User Messages** (`chat-route.ts`, lines 108-113):
```typescript
await transaction.create(userMessageRecord).content({
  conversation: conversationRecord,
  role: "user",
  text: userText,
  createdAt: now,
});
```

**Assistant Messages** (`chat-processor.ts`, lines 226-233):
```typescript
await input.deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
  conversation: conversationRecord,
  role: "assistant",
  text: assistantText,
  ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
  createdAt: now,
});
```

Messages are immutable after creation - no tool call data is stored with them.

---

### 8. Key Architectural Takeaway

The system separates concerns:
- **Messages** are text exchanges (user/assistant)
- **Tool calls** drive side effects (entity extraction, observations, graph mutations)
- **Entities** are the persistent knowledge graph
- **SSE events** stream real-time updates to the frontend
- **No tool invocation records** exist - only the results (extracted entities) are persisted

This is intentional: the focus is on what entities/knowledge were extracted, not the mechanics of how the agent got there.

Excellent! I have gathered comprehensive information about the subagent execution architecture. Let me now provide you with a detailed exploration report.

## Subagent Execution Architecture Analysis

I've thoroughly explored the codebase and can now provide you with a complete picture of the PM subagent execution architecture:

### 1. How `invoke_pm_agent` Tool Works

**File:** `/app/src/server/chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts`

The tool is a wrapper around `runPmAgent()` that:
- Takes input with `intent` (one of: `plan_work`, `check_status`, `organize`, `track_dependencies`), conversation context, and optional project scope
- Extracts the tool execution context via `requireToolContext()` to get workspace, conversation, and message records
- Calls `runPmAgent()` with dependencies and context
- Returns a `PmAgentOutput` or throws on error
- All errors are logged with workspace and conversation context

The tool description states: *"Invoke the PM subagent — the single authority on tasks, features, and project status."*

### 2. PM Agent Execution (Tool Loop Pattern)

**File:** `/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`

**Architecture:**
- Uses Vercel AI SDK's `ToolLoopAgent` class (not a streaming agent)
- **Step limit:** `stepCountIs(6)` - stops after max 6 tool invocations
- **Output schema:** Structured `pmAgentResultSchema` with:
  - `summary`: text summary of PM analysis
  - `suggestions`: array of `WorkItemSuggestion` (with kind, title, rationale, category, project, priority, possible_duplicate)
  - `updated`: existing items to merge with
  - `discarded`: items rejected with reasoning
  - `observations_created`: array of observation IDs created

**Execution Flow:**
```typescript
const agent = new ToolLoopAgent({
  id: "pm-agent",
  model: input.deps.pmAgentModel,  // Haiku (as per AGENTS.md)
  instructions: system,              // PM system prompt
  tools: createPmTools(input.deps),  // See below
  output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
  experimental_context: { ...input.context, actor: "pm_agent" as const },
  stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
});

const result = await agent.generate({
  prompt: [intent, context, conversation, instructions]
});
```

**Key behavior:**
- After execution, iterates through `result.steps` to collect `extracted_entities` and `extracted_relationships` from tool results
- Returns `PmAgentOutput` combining the structured result + collected entities/relationships
- **No streaming** - this is a non-streaming tool loop

### 3. PM Agent Tools

**File:** `/app/src/server/agents/pm/tools.ts`

The PM agent composes these shared tools:
```typescript
{
  search_entities,      // Vector search for workspace entities
  get_project_status,   // Aggregate task/decision/question counts
  create_observation,   // Create agent-to-agent signals (conflict/warning/info)
  create_suggestion,    // Agent-to-human proposals with confidence
  suggest_work_items,   // Batch triage with dedup (>0.97 exact, ≥0.8 merge, <0.8 new)
  create_work_item,     // Direct entity creation (task/feature/project)
}
```

**Important:** All these tools execute within the PM agent's tool loop. They write directly to the database during execution.

### 4. Data Returned from PM Agent to Chat Agent

**File:** `/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts` (lines 74-127)

```typescript
export type PmAgentOutput = PmAgentResult & {
  extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[];
  extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
};

export type PmAgentResult = {
  summary: string;
  suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[];
  updated: Array<{ existing_id: string; title: string; changes: string }>;
  discarded: Array<{ title: string; reason: string }>;
  observations_created: string[];
};
```

The chat agent receives:
1. **Structured output** (summary, suggestions, updated, discarded, observations_created)
2. **Collected entities** from tool results (extracted during PM execution)
3. **Collected relationships** from tool results (extracted during PM execution)

### 5. What Gets Stored in Database After PM Agent Execution

**During PM execution** (via tool invocations):
- `create_work_item`: Creates task/feature/project records with embedding, category, priority, provenance edges
- `create_observation`: Creates `observation` records (severity, status, category, source_agent)
- `create_suggestion`: Creates `suggestion` records with confidence and evidence links
- `suggest_work_items`: Returns triage results but **does NOT persist** them (dedup analysis only)

**After PM agent returns to chat agent** (in `chat-processor.ts` line 210):
```typescript
persistedEntities.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedEntities);
persistedRelationships.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedRelationships);
```

Then at line 248-253:
```typescript
input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
  type: "extraction",
  messageId: input.messageId,
  entities: persistedEntities,  // From PM agent + other sources
  relationships: persistedRelationships,
});
```

**Persistence via `persistExtractionOutput()`:**
- Entities and relationships are persisted to SurrealDB
- Embeddings are created and linked
- Provenance edges are created (extraction_relation)

### 6. SSE Event Flow During Subagent Execution

**File:** `/app/src/server/streaming/sse-registry.ts`

**Critical finding:** The SSE registry does **NOT stream during PM agent execution**.

**Event timeline:**
1. **Chat begins:** SSE stream registered, client connects
2. **Chat agent runs:** Streams:
   - `reasoning-delta` events → `onReasoning()` callback → SSE `reasoning` events
   - `text-delta` events → `onToken()` callback → SSE `token` events
   - `tool-call` events (logged, not streamed to client)
   - `tool-result` events (logged, entities collected)
3. **PM agent tool call:** When chat agent invokes `invoke_pm_agent`:
   - PM agent runs to completion (max 6 steps, non-streaming)
   - Tool results collected but **NO SSE events emitted during PM execution**
   - Returns structured output to chat agent
4. **After PM agent returns:**
   - Chat agent continues/completes response
   - Collects all entities (including PM-created ones)
   - **Single SSE `extraction` event** emitted at end (line 248-253) with all entities/relationships from entire message
5. **Message finalization:**
   - SSE `assistant_message` event (final text with optional suggestions)
   - SSE `onboarding_state` event (if onboarding changed)
   - SSE `done` event (closes stream)

**Discarded data:**
- PM agent's intermediate tool calls/results: logged but not exposed to client
- PM agent's step-by-step reasoning: never captured
- Work item triage analysis from `suggest_work_items`: returned as part of PM output but not separately streamed
- PM agent's internal decision logic: not exposed

### 7. Event Type Definitions

**File:** `/app/src/shared/contracts.ts`

Current `StreamEvent` types:
- `TokenEvent`: single token from chat agent output
- `ReasoningEvent`: chain-of-thought token
- `AssistantMessageEvent`: final assistant message text + suggestions
- `ExtractionEvent`: entities + relationships extracted
- `OnboardingSeedEvent`: onboarding entity suggestions
- `OnboardingStateEvent`: onboarding state transition
- `ObservationEvent`: observation created/acknowledged/resolved
- `DoneEvent`: stream complete
- `ErrorEvent`: stream error

**Missing event types for PM execution visibility:**
- No event for PM agent tool calls
- No event for PM agent results
- No event for PM agent reasoning steps
- No event for work item triage decisions
- No event for observations created by PM agent (must be discovered via graph queries)

---

## Summary: Data Visibility & Exposure Gaps

**Currently exposed during PM execution:**
- Final PM structured output (summary, suggestions, updated, discarded)
- Observations created (discoverable by client querying graph, not streamed)
- Work items created (in database immediately, not streamed as suggestions)

**Currently discarded/invisible:**
- PM agent tool invocations and their arguments
- PM agent tool results (except entities collected)
- PM agent reasoning/decision steps
- Work item suggestion triage analysis (similarity scores, dedup logic, merge reasoning)
- Observation creation details (only IDs returned, not summaries)
- Which suggestions were created vs suggested to user
- PM agent's confidence in its analysis
- Intermediate entity searches the PM agent performed
- Project status queries the PM agent ran

This represents significant "dark matter" in the PM agent execution that could be valuable for:
- Transparency/audit trails
- User understanding of why suggestions were made
- Observability/debugging
- Learning from PM agent decisions

The plan has been written. Here is a summary of the design, followed by the critical files for implementation.

## Design Summary

The plan uses a **trace-embedded-in-tool-result** approach rather than a custom SSE sideband. The PM agent switches from `ToolLoopAgent.generate()` to `ToolLoopAgent.stream()` and uses `experimental_onToolCallStart` / `experimental_onToolCallFinish` callbacks to collect a full execution trace (tool name, complete args, complete result, duration per step). This trace is returned as part of the `PmAgentOutput` and flows through the Vercel AI SDK wire protocol natively as the `invoke_pm_agent` tool result.

**Live streaming:** The frontend sees the `invoke_pm_agent` tool part transition from `input-available` ("Running...") to `output-available` (full trace available). Each tool step renders as its own expandable `<details>` element within an outer collapsible block.

**Page reload:** The trace is persisted on the `message` SurrealDB record in a `subagent_traces` field. On conversation load, it comes back via `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage` and is injected as `DataUIPart` (`type: "data-subagent_trace"`) into the UIMessage parts array. The same `<SubagentTraceBlock>` component renders both paths.

**Key trade-off:** Per-step real-time progress (seeing each PM agent tool call appear one-by-one) is deferred to a future enhancement. The PM agent runs max 6 Haiku tool steps (3-10 seconds), so showing all steps at once when complete is acceptable for v1.

The plan also includes removing the legacy `/api/chat/messages` SSE path as a separate follow-up task.

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts` - Core change: switch to stream(), add trace collection with callbacks
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/app/src/server/chat/chat-route.ts` - Extract traces from responseMessage.parts in onFinish, persist to DB
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/app/src/shared/contracts.ts` - Define SubagentToolStep/SubagentTrace types, extend WorkspaceBootstrapMessage
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/app/src/client/routes/chat-page.tsx` - Render SubagentTraceBlock for both live tool-result and reload data-part paths
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/schema/surreal-schema.surql` - Add subagent_traces field definitions to message table (SCHEMAFULL)

     1→import { ToolLoopAgent, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type ExtractedEntity, type ExtractedRelationship } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import type { ChatToolDeps, ChatToolExecutionContext } from "../../chat/tools/types";
     5→import { buildPmSystemPrompt } from "./prompt";
     6→import { createPmTools } from "./tools";
     7→
     8→const workItemSuggestionSchema = z
     9→  .object({
    10→    kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]),
    11→    title: z.string().min(1),
    12→    rationale: z.string().min(1),
    13→    category: z.enum(["none", ...ENTITY_CATEGORIES]),
    14→    project: z.enum(["none"]).or(z.string().min(1)),
    15→    priority: z.enum(["none", ...ENTITY_PRIORITIES]),
    16→    possible_duplicate: z.enum(["none"]).or(
    17→      z.object({
    18→        id: z.string().min(1),
    19→        name: z.string().min(1),
    20→        similarity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    21→      }),
    22→    ),
    23→  })
    24→  .transform((v) => ({
    25→    ...v,
    26→    category: v.category === "none" ? undefined : v.category,
    27→    project: v.project === "none" ? undefined : v.project,
    28→    priority: v.priority === "none" ? undefined : v.priority,
    29→    possible_duplicate: v.possible_duplicate === "none" ? undefined : v.possible_duplicate,
    30→  }));
    31→
    32→const pmAgentResultSchema = z.object({
    33→  summary: z.string().min(1),
    34→  suggestions: z.array(workItemSuggestionSchema),
    35→  updated: z.array(
    36→    z.object({
    37→      existing_id: z.string().min(1),
    38→      title: z.string().min(1),
    39→      changes: z.string().min(1),
    40→    }),
    41→  ),
    42→  discarded: z.array(
    43→    z.object({
    44→      title: z.string().min(1),
    45→      reason: z.string().min(1),
    46→    }),
    47→  ),
    48→  observations_created: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    49→});
    50→
    51→export type WorkItemSuggestion = z.infer<typeof workItemSuggestionSchema>;
    52→export type PmAgentResult = z.infer<typeof pmAgentResultSchema>;
    53→
    54→export type PmAgentOutput = PmAgentResult & {
    55→  extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[];
    56→  extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
    57→};
    58→
    59→export type PmAgentInput = {
    60→  deps: ChatToolDeps & { pmAgentModel: any };
    61→  context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
    62→  intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies";
    63→  conversationContext: string;
    64→  project?: string;
    65→};
    66→
    67→const INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS: Record<PmAgentInput["intent"], string> = {
    68→  check_status: "Primary action: call get_project_status when a project scope is available.",
    69→  plan_work: "Primary action: propose tasks/features/projects with suggest_work_items or create_work_item, deduping each item.",
    70→  track_dependencies: "Primary action: identify blockers/dependencies and create observations for high-risk paths.",
    71→  organize: "Primary action: organize work into clear, deduplicated next steps.",
    72→};
    73→
    74→export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
    75→  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    76→    surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    77→    workspaceRecord: input.context.workspaceRecord,
    78→  });
    79→
    80→  const agent = new ToolLoopAgent({
    81→    id: "pm-agent",
    82→    model: input.deps.pmAgentModel,
    83→    instructions: system,
    84→    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
    85→    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
    86→    experimental_context: { ...input.context, actor: "pm_agent" as const },
    87→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
    88→  });
    89→
    90→  const result = await agent.generate({
    91→    prompt: [
    92→      "You are handling a PM request.",
    93→      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,
    94→      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : "Project hint: not provided",
    95→      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],
    96→      "Context:",
    97→      input.conversationContext,
    98→    ].join("\n"),
    99→  });
   100→
   101→  if (!result.output) {
   102→    throw new Error("pm agent did not produce structured output");
   103→  }
   104→
   105→  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
   106→  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
   107→
   108→  for (const step of result.steps) {
   109→    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
   110→      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   111→      if (res) {
   112→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {
   113→          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));
   114→        }
   115→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {
   116→          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));
   117→        }
   118→      }
   119→    }
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  return {
   123→    ...result.output,
   124→    extracted_entities,
   125→    extracted_relationships,
   126→  };
   127→}
   128→

     1→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2→import { convertToModelMessages, stepCountIs, streamText, type UIMessage } from "ai";
     3→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     4→import type { OnboardingAction } from "../../shared/contracts";
     5→import { HttpError } from "../http/errors";
     6→import { elapsedMs, logError, logInfo, logWarn } from "../http/observability";
     7→import { jsonError } from "../http/response";
     8→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
     9→import type { ConversationRow, WorkspaceRow } from "../extraction/types";
    10→import { parseRecordIdString, getWorkspaceOwnerRecord } from "../graph/queries";
    11→import { resolveWorkspaceRecord } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    12→import { deriveMessageTitle, refreshConversationTouchedBy, maybeUpgradeConversationTitle } from "../workspace/conversation-sidebar";
    13→import { buildChatContext, buildSystemPrompt } from "./context";
    14→import { createChatAgentTools } from "./tools";
    15→import { transitionOnboardingState } from "../onboarding/onboarding-state";
    16→import { createEmbedding, persistEmbeddings } from "../extraction/embedding-writeback";
    17→import { loadBranchChain } from "./branch-chain";
    18→
    19→type ChatRequestBody = {
    20→  messages: UIMessage[];
    21→  workspaceId: string;
    22→  conversationId?: string;
    23→  onboardingAction?: OnboardingAction;
    24→  discussEntityId?: string;
    25→};
    26→
    27→const onboardingActions = new Set(["finalize_onboarding", "continue_onboarding"]);
    28→
    29→export function createChatRouteHandler(deps: ServerDependencies) {
    30→  return (request: Request) => handleChatRequest(deps, request);
    31→}
    32→
    33→async function handleChatRequest(deps: ServerDependencies, request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    34→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    35→
    36→  let body: ChatRequestBody;
    37→  try {
    38→    body = await request.json() as ChatRequestBody;
    39→  } catch {
    40→    return jsonError("Request body must be valid JSON", 400);
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  if (!body.workspaceId || body.workspaceId.trim().length === 0) {
    44→    return jsonError("workspaceId is required", 400);
    45→  }
    46→  if (!Array.isArray(body.messages) || body.messages.length === 0) {
    47→    return jsonError("messages array is required", 400);
    48→  }
    49→  if (body.onboardingAction && !onboardingActions.has(body.onboardingAction)) {
    50→    return jsonError("onboardingAction must be finalize_onboarding or continue_onboarding", 400);
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  const lastMessage = body.messages[body.messages.length - 1];
    54→  if (lastMessage.role !== "user") {
    55→    return jsonError("last message must be a user message", 400);
    56→  }
    57→
    58→  const userText = extractTextFromUIMessage(lastMessage);
    59→  if (userText.trim().length === 0) {
    60→    return jsonError("user message text is required", 400);
    61→  }
    62→
    63→  const conversationId = body.conversationId ?? randomUUID();
    64→  const messageId = randomUUID();
    65→  const userMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", randomUUID());
    66→
    67→  let workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    68→
    69→  try {
    70→    workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, body.workspaceId);
    71→    const workspace = await deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    72→    if (!workspace) {
    73→      throw new HttpError(404, `workspace not found: ${body.workspaceId}`);
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    const discussEntityTables = ["project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"] as const;
    77→    const discussesRecord = body.discussEntityId
    78→      ? parseRecordIdString(body.discussEntityId, [...discussEntityTables])
    79→      : undefined;
    80→
    81→    const now = new Date();
    82→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
    83→    const existingConversation = await deps.surreal.select<ConversationRow>(conversationRecord);
    84→
    85→    // Persist user message in transaction
    86→    const transaction = await deps.surreal.beginTransaction();
    87→    try {
    88→      if (existingConversation) {
    89→        if (!existingConversation.workspace) {
    90→          throw new HttpError(500, "conversation is missing workspace scope");
    91→        }
    92→        if (existingConversation.workspace.id !== workspaceRecord.id) {
    93→          throw new HttpError(400, "conversation scope does not match workspaceId");
    94→        }
    95→        await transaction.update(conversationRecord).merge({ updatedAt: now });
    96→      } else {
    97→        await transaction.create(conversationRecord).content({
    98→          createdAt: now,
    99→          updatedAt: now,
   100→          workspace: workspaceRecord,
   101→          title: deriveMessageTitle(userText),
   102→          title_source: "message",
   103→          ...(workspace.onboarding_complete ? {} : { source: "onboarding" }),
   104→          ...(discussesRecord ? { discusses: discussesRecord } : {}),
   105→        });
   106→      }
   107→
   108→      await transaction.create(userMessageRecord).content({
   109→        conversation: conversationRecord,
   110→        role: "user",
   111→        text: userText,
   112→        createdAt: now,
   113→      });
   114→
   115→      if (!workspace.onboarding_complete) {
   116→        await transaction.update(workspaceRecord).merge({
   117→          onboarding_turn_count: workspace.onboarding_turn_count + 1,
   118→          updated_at: now,
   119→        });
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      await transaction.commit();
   123→    } catch (error) {
   124→      await transaction.cancel();
   125→      throw error;
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    // Embed user message (fire-and-forget)
   129→    const userMessageEmbedding = await createEmbedding(deps.embeddingModel, deps.config.embeddingDimension, userText);
   130→    if (userMessageEmbedding) {
   131→      void deps.surreal
   132→        .query("UPDATE $record MERGE { embedding: $embedding };", {
   133→          record: userMessageRecord,
   134→          embedding: userMessageEmbedding,
   135→        })
   136→        .catch(() => undefined);
   137→    }
   138→
   139→    // Compute onboarding state
   140→    const onboardingAfter = await transitionOnboardingState({
   141→      surreal: deps.surreal,
   142→      workspaceRecord,
   143→      workspace,
   144→      onboardingAction: body.onboardingAction,
   145→      now,
   146→    });
   147→
   148→    // Load branch context
   149→    const branchChain = await loadBranchChain(deps.surreal, conversationId);
   150→    let inheritedEntityIds: RecordId[] | undefined;
   151→    if (branchChain.length > 0) {
   152→      const inheritedMsgIds = branchChain.map((b) => new RecordId("message", b));
   153→      if (inheritedMsgIds.length > 0) {
   154→        const [entityRows] = await deps.surreal
   155→          .query<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>(
   156→            "SELECT DISTINCT out FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` IN $msgIds LIMIT 30;",
   157→            { msgIds: inheritedMsgIds },
   158→          )
   159→          .collect<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>();
   160→        inheritedEntityIds = entityRows.map((r) => r.out);
   161→      }
   162→    }
   163→
   164→    const workspaceOwnerRecord = await getWorkspaceOwnerRecord({
   165→      surreal: deps.surreal,
   166→      workspaceRecord,
   167→    });
   168→
   169→    // Build chat context and system prompt
   170→    const context = await buildChatContext({
   171→      surreal: deps.surreal,
   172→      conversationRecord,
   173→      workspaceRecord,
   174→      ...(userMessageEmbedding ? { userMessageEmbedding } : {}),
   175→      ...(inheritedEntityIds && inheritedEntityIds.length > 0 ? { inheritedEntityIds } : {}),
   176→      ...(existingConversation?.discusses ? { discussesRecord: existingConversation.discusses } : {}),
   177→    });
   178→
   179→    const system = buildSystemPrompt(context, {
   180→      isOnboarding: onboardingAfter !== "complete",
   181→      onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   182→    });
   183→
   184→    // Convert UIMessages to model messages
   185→    const modelMessages = await convertToModelMessages(body.messages);
   186→
   187→    const tools = createChatAgentTools({
   188→      surreal: deps.surreal,
   189→      pmAgentModel: deps.pmAgentModel,
   190→      analyticsAgentModel: deps.analyticsAgentModel,
   191→      analyticsSurreal: deps.analyticsSurreal,
   192→      embeddingModel: deps.embeddingModel,
   193→      embeddingDimension: deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   194→      extractionModelId: deps.config.extractionModelId,
   195→      extractionModel: deps.extractionModel,
   196→      extractionStoreThreshold: deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   197→    });
   198→
   199→    // Stream the response
   200→    const result = streamText({
   201→      model: deps.chatAgentModel,
   202→      system,
   203→      messages: modelMessages,
   204→      tools,
   205→      experimental_context: {
   206→        actor: "chat_agent",
   207→        workspaceRecord,
   208→        conversationRecord,
   209→        currentMessageRecord: userMessageRecord,
   210→        latestUserText: userText,
   211→        ...(workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
   212→      },
   213→      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
   214→    });
   215→
   216→    logInfo("chat.route.streaming", "Streaming chat response", {
   217→      conversationId,
   218→      messageId,
   219→      workspaceId: body.workspaceId,
   220→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   221→    });
   222→
   223→    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse({
   224→      sendReasoning: true,
   225→      onFinish: async ({ responseMessage }) => {
   226→        const rawText = responseMessage.parts
   227→          .filter((p): p is Extract<typeof p, { type: "text" }> => p.type === "text")
   228→          .map((p) => p.text)
   229→          .join("\n");
   230→        const assistantText = rawText.trim().length > 0
   231→          ? rawText.trim()
   232→          : "I could not generate a response for that request.";
   233→
   234→        // Persist assistant message
   235→        const assistantMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", messageId);
   236→        await deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
   237→          conversation: conversationRecord,
   238→          role: "assistant",
   239→          text: assistantText,
   240→          createdAt: now,
   241→        });
   242→
   243→        await deps.surreal.update(conversationRecord).merge({ updatedAt: now });
   244→
   245→        // Post-response hooks
   246→        await refreshConversationTouchedBy(deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   247→        await maybeUpgradeConversationTitle(deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   248→
   249→        // Fire-and-forget: embeddings
   250→        void persistEmbeddings({
   251→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   252→          embeddingModel: deps.embeddingModel,
   253→          embeddingDimension: deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   254→          assistantMessageRecord,
   255→          assistantText,
   256→          entities: [],
   257→        }).catch(() => undefined);
   258→
   259→        logInfo("chat.route.completed", "Chat response completed", {
   260→          conversationId,
   261→          messageId,
   262→          workspaceId: body.workspaceId,
   263→          durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   264→        });
   265→      },
   266→      messageMetadata: ({ part }) => {
   267→        if (part.type === "finish") {
   268→          return { onboardingState: onboardingAfter, conversationId };
   269→        }
   270→        return { onboardingState: onboardingAfter, conversationId };
   271→      },
   272→    });
   273→  } catch (error) {
   274→    if (error instanceof HttpError) {
   275→      logWarn("chat.route.http_error", "Chat route failed with client-facing error", {
   276→        statusCode: error.status,
   277→      });
   278→      return jsonError(error.message, error.status);
   279→    }
   280→
   281→    logError("chat.route.failed", "Chat route failed", error, {
   282→      conversationId,
   283→      messageId,
   284→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   285→    });
   286→    return jsonError("chat processing failed", 500);
   287→  }
   288→}
   289→
   290→function extractTextFromUIMessage(message: UIMessage): string {
   291→  for (const part of message.parts) {
   292→    if (part.type === "text") {
   293→      return part.text;
   294→    }
   295→  }
   296→  return "";
   297→}
   298→

DEFINE FIELD updatedAt ON oauthConsent TYPE datetime;
DEFINE INDEX oauthConsent_clientId_userId ON oauthConsent FIELDS clientId, userId;

### Message table schema
# Message table schema

DEFINE TABLE message SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD conversation ON message TYPE record<conversation>;
DEFINE FIELD role ON message TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ["user", "assistant"];
DEFINE FIELD text ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD createdAt ON message TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD suggestions ON message TYPE option<array<string>>;
DEFINE FIELD clientMessageId ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON message TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE INDEX message_conversation_createdAt ON message FIELDS conversation, createdAt;
DEFINE INDEX idx_message_embedding ON message FIELDS embedding HNSW DIMENSION 1536 DIST COSINE;

DEFINE TABLE entity SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD kind ON entity TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ["task", "decision", "question"];
DEFINE FIELD text ON entity TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD confidence ON entity TYPE float ASSERT $value >= 0 AND $value <= 1;
DEFINE FIELD sourceMessage ON entity TYPE record<message>;
DEFINE FIELD createdAt ON entity TYPE datetime;
DEFINE INDEX entity_createdAt ON entity FIELDS createdAt;

DEFINE TABLE relationship SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD type ON relationship TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ["BELONGS_TO", "DEPENDS_ON"];

   150→  async function refreshSidebar(wsId: string) {

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/.claude/plans/inherited-brewing-flurry.md",
  "content": "# Subagent Tool Execution Collapsible\n\n## Context\n\nWhen the chat agent invokes `invoke_pm_agent`, the PM subagent runs up to 6 tool steps internally (search_entities, create_work_item, suggest_work_items, etc.). Today, the user only sees `invoke_pm_agent: Running... → Done` — the subagent's internal work is invisible. The goal is to show these internal steps in a collapsible `<details>` block (like the existing \"Thinking\" block for reasoning).\n\n**Two problems to solve:**\n1. **Live streaming**: Show subagent steps as they complete during the current message\n2. **Page reload**: Reconstruct the collapsible from persisted data when loading a conversation\n\n## Architecture Decision\n\n**Piggyback on tool result** — The Vercel AI SDK already streams `invoke_pm_agent` tool results to the frontend as `tool-invoke_pm_agent` parts. By adding a `trace` field to the `PmAgentOutput`, the steps flow to the frontend naturally with zero new SSE events. The frontend renders the trace inside the existing tool-part handler.\n\n**Store on message record** — A new `subagent_traces` field on the `message` table stores traces for replay on reload. This is simpler than a separate table since traces are 1:1 with assistant messages and small (max 6 steps × ~200 bytes).\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. Define trace types (`app/src/shared/contracts.ts`)\n\n```ts\nexport type SubagentToolStep = {\n  toolName: string;\n  args: Record<string, unknown>;  // tool input (may be truncated for large payloads)\n  durationMs: number;\n};\n\nexport type SubagentTrace = {\n  agentId: string;        // \"pm_agent\"\n  intent: string;         // \"plan_work\", \"check_status\", etc.\n  steps: SubagentToolStep[];\n  totalDurationMs: number;\n};\n```\n\nAdd `subagentTraces?: SubagentTrace[]` to `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage`.\n\n### 2. Collect trace in PM agent (`app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`)\n\nAdd a `trace` field to `PmAgentOutput`. After `agent.generate()`, iterate `result.steps` to build the trace:\n\n```ts\nconst steps: SubagentToolStep[] = [];\nfor (const step of result.steps) {\n  for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {\n    steps.push({\n      toolName: toolResult.toolName,\n      args: toolResult.args,\n      durationMs: toolResult.durationMs ?? 0,\n    });\n  }\n}\n```\n\nReturn the trace alongside existing output. No changes to the tool loop itself.\n\n### 3. Persist trace on message (`app/src/server/chat/chat-route.ts`)\n\nIn the `onFinish` callback, extract subagent traces from `responseMessage.parts` (tool-result parts for `invoke_pm_agent`) and persist them on the assistant message record:\n\n```ts\n// In onFinish:\nconst subagentTraces = responseMessage.parts\n  .filter(p => p.type === 'tool-result' && p.toolName === 'invoke_pm_agent')\n  .map(p => p.result?.trace)\n  .filter(Boolean);\n\nawait deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({\n  // ...existing fields...\n  ...(subagentTraces.length > 0 ? { subagent_traces: subagentTraces } : {}),\n});\n```\n\n### 4. Schema migration (`schema/migrations/NNNN_message_subagent_traces.surql`)\n\n```surql\nBEGIN TRANSACTION;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces ON message TYPE option<array<object>>;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].agentId ON message TYPE string;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].intent ON message TYPE string;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps ON message TYPE array<object>;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].toolName ON message TYPE string;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].args ON message TYPE object;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].durationMs ON message TYPE int;\nDEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].totalDurationMs ON message TYPE int;\nCOMMIT TRANSACTION;\n```\n\n### 5. Include traces in conversation load (`app/src/server/workspace/workspace-routes.ts`)\n\nUpdate message SELECT queries to include `subagent_traces`. Map into `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage.subagentTraces`.\n\nAlso update `branch-chain.ts` message queries to include the field.\n\n### 6. Reconstruct tool parts on reload (`app/src/client/hooks/use-chat-session.ts`)\n\nUpdate `bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages` to inject tool parts for messages with traces:\n\n```ts\nfunction bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(messages: WorkspaceBootstrapMessage[]): ChatUIMessage[] {\n  return messages.map((m) => {\n    const parts: UIMessagePart[] = [{ type: \"text\", text: m.text }];\n    if (m.subagentTraces?.length) {\n      for (const trace of m.subagentTraces) {\n        parts.push({\n          type: \"tool-invoke_pm_agent\",\n          state: \"output-available\",\n          output: { trace },\n        } as any);\n      }\n    }\n    return { id: m.id, role: m.role, parts, createdAt: new Date(m.createdAt) };\n  });\n}\n```\n\n### 7. Render collapsible (`app/src/client/routes/chat-page.tsx`)\n\nIn the tool-part rendering branch, add handling for `invoke_pm_agent`:\n\n```tsx\nif (toolName === \"invoke_pm_agent\" && toolPart.state === \"output-available\") {\n  const trace = (toolPart.output as any)?.trace as SubagentTrace;\n  if (trace) {\n    return (\n      <details key={i} className=\"subagent-trace-block\">\n        <summary>\n          PM Agent ({trace.steps.length} steps, {(trace.totalDurationMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s)\n        </summary>\n        <div className=\"subagent-trace-steps\">\n          {trace.steps.map((step, j) => (\n            <div key={j} className=\"subagent-trace-step\">\n              <span className=\"subagent-trace-tool\">{step.toolName}</span>\n              <span className=\"subagent-trace-duration\">{step.durationMs}ms</span>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      </details>\n    );\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Files to modify\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `app/src/shared/contracts.ts` | Add `SubagentToolStep`, `SubagentTrace` types; extend `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage` |\n| `app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts` | Collect trace from `result.steps`, add to `PmAgentOutput` |\n| `app/src/server/chat/chat-route.ts` | Extract traces from response parts in `onFinish`, persist on message |\n| `schema/migrations/NNNN_*.surql` | Add `subagent_traces` field to message table |\n| `schema/surreal-schema.surql` | Update canonical schema with new field |\n| `app/src/server/workspace/workspace-routes.ts` | Include `subagent_traces` in message SELECT queries |\n| `app/src/server/chat/branch-chain.ts` | Include `subagent_traces` in message queries |\n| `app/src/client/hooks/use-chat-session.ts` | Reconstruct tool parts from traces on reload |\n| `app/src/client/routes/chat-page.tsx` | Render `SubagentTraceBlock` collapsible |\n| `app/src/client/styles/chat.css` (or equivalent) | Styles for trace block |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Reload page → verify collapsible reconstructs from DB\n5. Branch conversation → verify traces carry through inherited messages\n"
}

## Vercel AI SDK ToolLoopAgent & streamText Type Research

Based on my exploration of the Vercel AI SDK type definitions and existing code patterns in your codebase, here's the complete structure:

### 1. **ToolLoopAgent.generate() Result Structure**

**Return Type:** `GenerateTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>`

```typescript
interface GenerateTextResult<TOOLS extends ToolSet, OUTPUT extends Output> {
  // Last step content
  readonly content: Array<ContentPart<TOOLS>>;
  readonly text: string;
  readonly reasoning: Array<ReasoningOutput>;
  readonly reasoningText: string | undefined;
  readonly files: Array<GeneratedFile>;
  readonly sources: Array<Source>;
  
  // Tool invocations (last step only)
  readonly toolCalls: Array<TypedToolCall<TOOLS>>;
  readonly staticToolCalls: Array<StaticToolCall<TOOLS>>;
  readonly dynamicToolCalls: Array<DynamicToolCall>;
  
  // Tool results (last step only)
  readonly toolResults: Array<TypedToolResult<TOOLS>>;
  readonly staticToolResults: Array<StaticToolResult<TOOLS>>;
  readonly dynamicToolResults: Array<DynamicToolResult>;
  
  // Completion state
  readonly finishReason: FinishReason; // 'stop' | 'length' | 'content-filter' | 'tool-calls' | 'error' | 'other'
  readonly rawFinishReason: string | undefined;
  
  // Token usage
  readonly usage: LanguageModelUsage;
  readonly totalUsage: LanguageModelUsage; // Sum of all steps
  
  // Warnings & metadata
  readonly warnings: CallWarning[] | undefined;
  readonly request: LanguageModelRequestMetadata;
  readonly response: LanguageModelResponseMetadata & {
    readonly messages: Array<ResponseMessage>; // AssistantModelMessage | ToolModelMessage
    readonly body?: unknown;
  };
  readonly providerMetadata: ProviderMetadata | undefined;
  
  // ALL STEPS (key for tracing!)
  readonly steps: Array<StepResult<TOOLS>>;
  
  // Structured output
  readonly output: InferCompleteOutput<OUTPUT>;
  readonly experimental_output: InferCompleteOutput<OUTPUT>; // @deprecated
}
```

### 2. **StepResult Structure (Each Element in result.steps)**

```typescript
type StepResult<TOOLS extends ToolSet> = {
  // Step metadata
  readonly stepNumber: number; // Zero-based index
  readonly model: {
    readonly provider: string; // e.g., 'openai'
    readonly modelId: string;
  };
  readonly functionId: string | undefined;
  readonly metadata: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  readonly experimental_context: unknown;
  
  // Step content
  readonly content: Array<ContentPart<TOOLS>>;
  readonly text: string;
  readonly reasoning: Array<ReasoningPart>;
  readonly reasoningText: string | undefined;
  readonly files: Array<GeneratedFile>;
  readonly sources: Array<Source>;
  
  // Tool invocations in THIS step
  readonly toolCalls: Array<TypedToolCall<TOOLS>>;
  readonly staticToolCalls: Array<StaticToolCall<TOOLS>>;
  readonly dynamicToolCalls: Array<DynamicToolCall>;
  
  // Tool results for THIS step
  readonly toolResults: Array<TypedToolResult<TOOLS>>;
  readonly staticToolResults: Array<StaticToolResult<TOOLS>>;
  readonly dynamicToolResults: Array<DynamicToolResult>;
  
  // Completion
  readonly finishReason: FinishReason;
  readonly rawFinishReason: string | undefined;
  
  // Usage for this step only
  readonly usage: LanguageModelUsage;
  
  // Warnings & metadata
  readonly warnings: CallWarning[] | undefined;
  readonly request: LanguageModelRequestMetadata;
  readonly response: LanguageModelResponseMetadata & {
    readonly messages: Array<ResponseMessage>;
    readonly body?: unknown;
  };
  readonly providerMetadata: ProviderMetadata | undefined;
};
```

### 3. **ToolResult Structure (Each Element in step.toolResults)**

**Union Type:** `TypedToolResult<TOOLS> = StaticToolResult<TOOLS> | DynamicToolResult`

**StaticToolResult** (typed tools):
```typescript
type StaticToolResult<TOOLS extends ToolSet> = {
  type: 'tool-result';
  toolCallId: string;
  toolName: string; // Typed to tool names in TOOLS
  input: InferToolInput<TOOLS[toolName]>;
  output: InferToolOutput<TOOLS[toolName]>; // The actual result data
  providerExecuted?: boolean;
  providerMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
  dynamic?: false | undefined;
  preliminary?: boolean; // Streaming result not yet final
  title?: string;
};
```

**DynamicToolResult** (untyped/dynamic tools):
```typescript
type DynamicToolResult = {
  type: 'tool-result';
  toolCallId: string;
  toolName: string;
  input: unknown;
  output: unknown; // The actual result data
  providerExecuted?: boolean;
  providerMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
  dynamic: true;
  preliminary?: boolean;
  title?: string;
};
```

**Key Point:** No `durationMs` field on tool results themselves. Duration must be calculated from `response.timestamp` if needed.

### 4. **streamText + toUIMessageStreamResponse Flow**

**streamText Return Type:** `StreamTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>`

**toUIMessageStreamResponse Signature:**
```typescript
toUIMessageStreamResponse<UI_MESSAGE extends UIMessage>(
  options?: UIMessageStreamResponseInit & UIMessageStreamOptions<UI_MESSAGE>
): Response;
```

**onFinish Callback:**
```typescript
type UIMessageStreamOnFinishCallback<UI_MESSAGE extends UIMessage> = (event: {
  /**
   * The final list of UI messages after streaming completes
   */
  messages: UI_MESSAGE[];
  
  /**
   * Whether this response was appended to the last assistant message
   * or created as a new message
   */
  isContinuation: boolean;
  
  /**
   * Whether the stream was aborted
   */
  isAborted: boolean;
  
  /**
   * The response message with all parts
   * (this is what you inspect for tool calls/results)
   */
  responseMessage: UI_MESSAGE;
  
  /**
   * Why generation finished
   */
  finishReason?: FinishReason;
}) => PromiseLike<void> | void;
```

### 5. **responseMessage.parts Structure**

**Type:** `Array<UIMessagePart<DATA_TYPES, TOOLS>>`

**Possible Part Types:**

1. **Text Part:**
   ```typescript
   type TextUIPart = {
     type: 'text';
     text: string;
     state?: 'streaming' | 'done';
     providerMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
   };
   ```

2. **Reasoning Part:**
   ```typescript
   type ReasoningUIPart = {
     type: 'reasoning';
     text: string;
     state?: 'streaming' | 'done';
     providerMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
   };
   ```

3. **Tool Invocation (Typed):**
   ```typescript
   type ToolUIPart<TOOLS> = {
     type: `tool-${NAME}`; // e.g., 'tool-search_entities'
     toolCallId: string;
     title?: string;
     providerExecuted?: boolean;
     callProviderMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
     // State + input/output based on execution state:
   } & UIToolInvocation<TOOLS[NAME]>;
   ```

4. **Tool Invocation (Untyped/Dynamic):**
   ```typescript
   type DynamicToolUIPart = {
     type: 'dynamic-tool';
     toolName: string;
     toolCallId: string;
     title?: string;
     providerExecuted?: boolean;
     state: 'input-streaming' | 'input-available' | 'approval-requested' 
          | 'approval-responded' | 'output-available' | 'output-error' | 'output-denied';
     input: unknown;
     output?: unknown; // Only present if state is 'output-available'
     errorText?: string; // Only if state is 'output-error'
     callProviderMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
   };
   ```

5. **Source Parts, File Parts, etc.**

### 6. **UIToolInvocation State Transitions**

Tool invocations in `responseMessage.parts` progress through states:

```typescript
type UIToolInvocation<TOOL> = {
  toolCallId: string;
  title?: string;
  providerExecuted?: boolean;
} & (
  // Input is being streamed in
  | {
      state: 'input-streaming';
      input: DeepPartial<TOOL['input']> | undefined;
      output?: never;
      errorText?: never;
      callProviderMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
    }
  // Input is complete, ready to execute
  | {
      state: 'input-available';
      input: TOOL['input'];
      output?: never;
      errorText?: never;
      callProviderMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
    }
  // Tool execution completed successfully
  | {
      state: 'output-available';
      input: TOOL['input'];
      output: TOOL['output'];
      errorText?: never;
      callProviderMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
      preliminary?: boolean; // Not yet finalized
    }
  // Tool execution failed
  | {
      state: 'output-error';
      input: TOOL['input'] | undefined;
      rawInput?: unknown;
      output?: never;
      errorText: string;
      callProviderMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
    }
  // Tool call was denied (approval-required tool rejected)
  | {
      state: 'output-denied';
      input: TOOL['input'];
      output?: never;
      errorText?: never;
      callProviderMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
    }
  // ... and approval states for tools requiring approval
);
```

### 7. **Key Data Available for Traces**

From `result.steps[i]`:
- **stepNumber** (zero-indexed)
- **model.provider, model.modelId**
- **toolCalls/toolResults** (typed and dynamic)
- **usage** (input/output tokens, reasoning tokens if applicable)
- **finishReason** (whether stopped by tool calls or model completion)
- **response.messages** (raw assistant + tool messages)
- **response.timestamp, response.id, response.modelId** (timing metadata)

From `responseMessage.parts` (in onFinish callback):
- **Tool parts with states** (input-streaming → input-available → output-available)
- **Text parts** (final response text)
- **Reasoning parts** (if sendReasoning: true)

### 8. **Current Usage Pattern in Your Codebase**

```typescript
// PM Agent (app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts:108-120)
for (const step of result.steps) {
  for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
    const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    if (res?.extracted_entities) {
      extracted_entities.push(...res.extracted_entities);
    }
  }
}

// Chat Route (app/src/server/chat/chat-route.ts:226-229)

  .filter((p): p is Extract<typeof p, { type: "text" }> => p.type === "text")
  .map((p) => p.text)
  .join("\n");
```

## Summary for Tracing

**To collect comprehensive traces:**

1. **Use `result.steps`** (from `agent.generate()`) to capture:
   - All intermediate reasoning and tool calls
   - Per-step token usage
   - Exact tool inputs/outputs

2. **Use `onFinish({ responseMessage })`** (from `streamText().toUIMessageStreamResponse()`) to capture:
   - Final UI-renderable parts
   - Tool invocation states with full lifecycle
   - Final text and reasoning output

3. **Combine both** for full visibility:
   - Steps show LLM-level details (completions, tool results)
   - responseMessage parts show client-facing representation with state transitions

4. **No durationMs on tool results** — calculate from timestamps or use `performance.now()` at tool invocation boundaries if needed.

## Vercel AI SDK UIMessage Stream Protocol - Complete Research Report

Based on my analysis of the Vercel AI SDK type definitions and your codebase implementation, here are the answers to your questions:

---

### 1. **Tool Call Flow in `toUIMessageStreamResponse()`**

When you use `streamText().toUIMessageStreamResponse()` in `app/src/server/chat/chat-route.ts`, tool calls flow to the client through the **UIMessageChunk stream protocol**:

**Stream Events (in order):**
- `tool-input-available`: Server sends when the tool name/input are known
  ```typescript
  {
    type: 'tool-input-available';
    toolCallId: string;
    toolName: string;
    input: unknown;
    providerExecuted?: boolean;
    providerMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
  }
  ```

- `tool-output-available`: Server sends when tool completes
  ```typescript
  {
    type: 'tool-output-available';
    toolCallId: string;
    output: unknown;  // ← Tool result goes here
    providerExecuted?: boolean;
    preliminary?: boolean;
  }
  ```

- `tool-input-error` or `tool-output-error`: If tool fails
  ```typescript
  {
    type: 'tool-input-error' | 'tool-output-error';
    toolCallId: string;
    toolName?: string;
    errorText: string;
  }
  ```

**Are they separate events?** Yes. The stream sends `tool-input-available` first, then later `tool-output-available` as separate chunks.

---

### 2. **What `useChat` Hook Receives**

The `@ai-sdk/react` `useChat` hook processes these chunks and **reconstructs them into typed `UIMessage.parts`** on the client.

**Client-side `message.parts` for a tool invocation contains:**
```typescript
type ToolUIPart<TOOLS> = {
  type: `tool-${TOOL_NAME}`;  // e.g., "tool-invoke_pm_agent"
} & UIToolInvocation<TOOLS[NAME]>;

// UIToolInvocation has these variants:
{
  state: 'input-available';
  toolCallId: string;
  input: unknown;  // The tool input args
  output?: never;
} |
{
  state: 'output-available';
  toolCallId: string;
  input: unknown;
  output: unknown;  // ← Tool result here!
  preliminary?: boolean;
} |
{
  state: 'output-error';
  toolCallId: string;
  errorText: string;
}
```

**Your frontend code checks this:**
```typescript
if (isToolPart(part)) {
  const toolPart = part as { type: string; state: string; output?: unknown };
  const toolName = toolPart.type.replace(/^tool-/, "");
  
  if (toolPart.state === "output-available" && toolPart.output) {
    // ✓ Output is accessible here as toolPart.output
  }
}
```

---

### 3. **In the `onFinish` Callback**

When `toUIMessageStreamResponse()` calls its `onFinish` callback:

```typescript
onFinish: async ({ responseMessage }) => {
  // responseMessage is a fully-constructed UIMessage
  const parts = responseMessage.parts;  // UIMessagePart[]
```

**What `responseMessage.parts` contains:**
- `TextUIPart` (type: 'text') - text generated by the model
- `ReasoningUIPart` (type: 'reasoning') - thinking blocks
- `ToolUIPart` (type: 'tool-{name}') - **YES, tool parts ARE included** with:
  - `state: 'output-available'` (if completed)
  - `output: unknown` (the actual tool result)
- Other parts: `SourceUrlUIPart`, `FileUIPart`, etc.

**Your code correctly extracts text:**
```typescript
const rawText = responseMessage.parts
  .filter((p): p is Extract<typeof p, { type: "text" }> => p.type === "text")
  .map((p) => p.text)
  .join("\n");
```

**To access tool outputs from `onFinish`:**
```typescript
responseMessage.parts
  .filter((p) => p.type.startsWith("tool-"))
  .forEach((toolPart) => {
    if (toolPart.state === "output-available") {
      console.log(toolPart.output);  // ← Tool result
    }
  });
```

---

### 4. **`useChat` Hook Tool Handling**

The `useChat` hook from `@ai-sdk/react` has these capabilities:

**Tool Invocation Callback:**
```typescript
onToolCall?: ChatOnToolCallCallback<UI_MESSAGE> = (options: {
  toolCall: InferUIMessageToolCall<UI_MESSAGE>;
}) => void | PromiseLike<void>;
```

This is **NOT currently used** in your `use-chat-session.ts`:
```typescript
const chat = useChat<ChatUIMessage>({
  transport,
  onFinish: ({ message }) => { ... },  // ← You use onFinish
  onError: (error) => { ... },
  // ✗ No onToolCall - tools are auto-executed server-side
});
```

**Part Types in `message.parts`:**
- `text` - text content
- `reasoning` - extended thinking
- `tool-{NAME}` - tool invocation (dynamic type based on tool name)
- `dynamic-tool` - fallback for unknown tools
- `source-url`, `source-document`, `file` - attachments
- `data` - custom data parts

---

### 5. **Nested Tool Calls (PM Agent)**

For `invoke_pm_agent` (which itself runs an agent with tools), the client sees:

```typescript
// In message.parts:
{
  type: "tool-invoke_pm_agent";
  state: "output-available";
  toolCallId: "...",
  input: { intent: "plan_work", ... };
  output: {
    // PmAgentOutput structure
    summary: string;
    suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[];
    updated: number;
    discarded: number;
    observations_created: number;
  }
}
```

**Your rendering in chat-page.tsx:**
```typescript
if (toolName === "create_suggestion" && toolPart.state === "output-available" && toolPart.output) {
  const output = toolPart.output as { text: string; category: string; ... };
  return <SuggestionToolCard key={i} output={output} />;
}
```

This pattern works the same for `invoke_pm_agent`. The nested agent's output appears as a single `tool-invoke_pm_agent` part with `state: "output-available"`.

---

### 6. **Stream Message Sequence (Complete)**

Here's the exact sequence when `toUIMessageStreamResponse()` streams a response with a tool call:

```
1. { type: 'start', messageId, messageMetadata }
2. { type: 'text-start', id }
3. { type: 'text-delta', delta: "Planning..." }
4. { type: 'text-end', id }
5. { type: 'tool-input-available', toolCallId, toolName: 'invoke_pm_agent', input: {...} }
6. { type: 'tool-output-available', toolCallId, output: {...} }
7. { type: 'text-start', id }
8. { type: 'text-delta', delta: "Here are..." }
9. { type: 'text-end', id }
10. { type: 'finish', finishReason: 'stop', messageMetadata }
```

The `useChat` hook reconstructs these into a single `UIMessage`:
```typescript
{
  id: "msg-123",
  role: "assistant",
  metadata: { onboardingState: "complete", conversationId: "..." },
  parts: [
    { type: "text", text: "Planning..." },
    { type: "tool-invoke_pm_agent", state: "output-available", toolCallId: "...", input: {...}, output: {...} },
    { type: "text", text: "Here are..." }
  ]
}
```

---

### Key Takeaways:

1. ✓ Tool calls and results are **separate stream chunks** (`tool-input-available` → `tool-output-available`)
2. ✓ `useChat` reconstructs them into typed `ToolUIPart` objects with `state` and `output`
3. ✓ `onFinish({ responseMessage })` receives the **complete message** with all parts including tool results
4. ✓ You can extract tool outputs from `responseMessage.parts` in `onFinish`
5. ✓ Nested agents (PM agent with its own tools) appear as a single tool invocation with the agent's output
6. ✓ The `isToolPart()` check works correctly for all tool types

Your current implementation correctly passes through tool results in the chat stream, and the frontend properly renders them via the `isToolPart()` function.

## Research Report: SurrealDB SCHEMAFULL Handling of Deeply Nested Objects

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here are the key findings:

### 1. **How Nested `array<object>` Fields Are Currently Defined**

The schema uses explicit nested field declarations for all `array<object>` types. Here are the real examples:

**description_entries pattern** (project, feature, task tables):
```sql
DEFINE FIELD description_entries ON project TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD description_entries[*].text ON project TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD description_entries[*].source ON project TYPE option<record>;
DEFINE FIELD description_entries[*].created_at ON project TYPE datetime;
```

The actual TypeScript type:
```typescript
export type DescriptionEntry = {
  text: string;
  source?: RecordId;
  created_at: Date;
};
```

**tasks_progressed pattern** (agent_session table):
```sql
DEFINE FIELD tasks_progressed ON agent_session TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD tasks_progressed[*].task_id ON agent_session TYPE record<task>;
DEFINE FIELD tasks_progressed[*].from_status ON agent_session TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD tasks_progressed[*].to_status ON agent_session TYPE string;
```

**files_changed pattern** (agent_session table):
```sql
DEFINE FIELD files_changed ON agent_session TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD files_changed[*].path ON agent_session TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD files_changed[*].change_type ON agent_session TYPE string
  ASSERT $value IN ["created", "modified", "deleted"];
```

**identities pattern** (person table):
```sql
DEFINE FIELD identities ON person TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD identities[*].provider ON person TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD identities[*].id ON person TYPE string;
```

### 2. **SCHEMAFULL Flexibility for Arbitrary JSON**

**Answer: NO.** SCHEMAFULL does NOT allow arbitrary JSON in object fields. 

- There is no `TYPE any` in SurrealDB
- There is no `TYPE object` that accepts unknown keys
- Every nested key must be explicitly declared in the schema
- SCHEMAFULL silently rejects writes with unknown keys — they don't appear in the database

This is enforced at the schema layer, not at query time, making it a silent failure mode.

### 3. **The AGENTS.md Rule in Action**

From AGENTS.md:
> "In SCHEMAFULL, every persisted nested key must be explicitly declared. For `array<object>` fields, always define `field[*].subField` entries for all written properties."

This codebase **strictly adheres** to this rule. Every nested object field has all its sub-properties declared upfront via `DEFINE FIELD field[*].subField` statements.

### 4. **Solution for Variable-Shape Tool Args/Results**

Given that each tool has different input/output schemas, here are the viable patterns:

**Option A: JSON String Storage (Recommended for variable shapes)**
```sql
-- Store args and result as JSON strings
DEFINE FIELD tool_args ON tool_invocation TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD tool_result ON tool_invocation TYPE option<string>;
```

Then in TypeScript:
```typescript
const invocation = {
  tool_name: "search_entities",
  tool_args: JSON.stringify({ query: "...", limit: 20 }),
  tool_result: JSON.stringify({ results: [...] }),
};
```

**Pros:**
- Flexible: any JSON shape, any tool
- No schema changes per tool
- Standard pattern for extensible data
- Query-able with SurrealDB's `json::*` functions

**Cons:**
- Can't index on nested keys
- Requires parsing in TypeScript
- SurrealDB treats it as text, not a structured object

**Option B: Fixed Schema (Current pattern used in this codebase)**
Create a migration adding explicit schema for each tool's args/result shape:
```sql
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations ON agent_session TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].tool_name ON agent_session TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].args ON agent_session TYPE option<object>;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].args.query ON agent_session TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].args.limit ON agent_session TYPE option<int>;
-- ... more fields for other tools
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].result ON agent_session TYPE option<object>;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].result.items ON agent_session TYPE option<array<object>>;
-- ... more fields
```

**Pros:**
- Fully typed and queryable
- SurrealDB enforces schema
- Aligns with project conventions
- Good for known, limited tools

**Cons:**
- Schema explosion as tools grow
- Requires migration per new tool
- Breaks on unexpected fields
- Can't handle dynamic tool args

**Option C: Hybrid Approach (Best of both)**
```sql
-- Nullable typed fields for known tools, plus generic JSON fallback
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations ON agent_session TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].tool_name ON agent_session TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].tool_args_json ON agent_session TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD tool_invocations[*].tool_result_json ON agent_session TYPE option<string>;
```

This allows storage of any tool without schema changes, while keeping the structured metadata (tool_name) queryable.

### 5. **How This Codebase Currently Handles Variable Data**

The codebase currently:
- **Does NOT store arbitrary tool invocations** in the database
- **Uses graph relations instead** for structured data (e.g., `progressed` relation in agent_session to track task status changes)
- **Follows the "describe all nested keys" pattern** for semi-variable data (description_entries, tasks_progressed, files_changed)
- **Never uses JSON stringify** for domain data meant to be queryable

### Key Architectural Pattern

Rather than storing "tool invocation history" as a flexible object, this codebase:
1. Extracts structured results (task status change)
2. Creates explicit relations in the graph (`progressed` edge from agent_session to task)
3. Stores metadata on the relation (`from_status`, `to_status`)
4. Queries via graph traversal, not nested object lookups

This keeps the schema strongly typed and query-able while maintaining flexibility through graph relations.

---

**Recommendation for tool storage:**
If you need to log tool invocations with variable args/results, use **Option C (Hybrid)** with JSON string fields for args/results. This maintains queryability of the tool name and timestamps while accepting any tool schema without migrations.
agentId: ab9fea9eb8c55c03d (for resuming to continue this agent's work if needed)
<usage>total_tokens: 70847
tool_uses: 19
duration_ms: 112981</usage>
```

3. **`step.text` confirmed available** — each `StepResult` has `text` for intermediate PM agent messages.
4. **`toolResult.input`** not `toolResult.args` — different field name than I had in the plan.

Collect trace in PM agent (`app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`)\n\nAdd a `trace` field to `PmAgentOutput`. Wrap `agent.generate()` with `performance.now()` for total duration. After completion, iterate `result.steps` to build the trace:\n\n```ts\nconst startedAt = performance.now();\nconst result = await agent.generate({ prompt: ... });\nconst totalDurationMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);\n\nconst traceSteps: SubagentTraceStep[] = [];\nfor (const step of result.steps) {\n  // Capture intermediate text (PM agent reasoning between tool calls)\n  if (step.text?.trim()) {\n    traceSteps.push({ type: \"text\", text: step.text.trim() });\n  }\n  // Capture tool invocations with full args and results\n  for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {\n    traceSteps.push({\n      type: \"tool_call\",\n      toolName: toolResult.toolName,\n      argsJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.input),\n      resultJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.output),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\nconst trace: SubagentTrace = {\n  agentId: \"pm_agent\",\n  intent: input.intent,\n  steps: traceSteps,\n  totalDurationMs,\n};\n```\n\nNote: Per-tool `durationMs` is omitted in v1 since the SDK doesn't expose it. `totalDurationMs` covers the whole agent run. Per-step timing could be added later by wrapping tool `execute` functions.\n\n### 3.
