## Approved Plan:
# Subagent Tool Execution Collapsible

## Context

When the chat agent invokes `invoke_pm_agent`, the PM subagent runs up to 6 tool steps internally (search_entities, create_work_item, suggest_work_items, etc.). Today, the user only sees `invoke_pm_agent: Running... → Done` — the subagent's internal work is invisible. The goal is to show these internal steps in a collapsible `<details>` block (like the existing "Thinking" block for reasoning).

**Two problems to solve:**
1. **Live streaming**: Show subagent steps as they complete during the current message
2. **Page reload**: Reconstruct the collapsible from persisted data when loading a conversation

## Architecture Decision

**Piggyback on tool result** — The Vercel AI SDK already streams `invoke_pm_agent` tool results to the frontend as `tool-invoke_pm_agent` parts (state transitions: `input-available` → `output-available`). By adding a `trace` field to `PmAgentOutput`, the steps flow to the frontend naturally with zero new SSE events. The frontend renders the trace inside the existing tool-part handler.

**Store on message record** — A new `subagent_traces` field on the `message` table stores traces for replay on reload. This is simpler than a separate table since traces are 1:1 with assistant messages and small (max 6 steps).

## Research Findings (AI SDK + SurrealDB)

### AI SDK `StepResult` structure
- `step.text` — intermediate text output (PM agent reasoning between tools). Confirmed available.
- `step.toolResults[i].input` — tool input args (NOT `.args`).
- `step.toolResults[i].output` — tool result output.
- `step.toolResults[i].toolName` — tool name string.
- **No `durationMs`** on tool results. Must measure manually with `performance.now()` or derive from `step.response.timestamp` deltas.

### AI SDK `onFinish` parts
- `responseMessage.parts` includes tool parts typed as `"tool-invoke_pm_agent"` (not `"tool-result"`).
- Access: `part.type === "tool-invoke_pm_agent"`, `part.state === "output-available"`, `part.output` = full `PmAgentOutput`.

### SurrealDB SCHEMAFULL constraint
- `TYPE object` silently drops keys not declared via `DEFINE FIELD`. Tool args/results have variable shapes per tool.
- **Solution**: Store `argsJson` and `resultJson` as `TYPE string` (JSON-stringified). Keeps schema stable across tool changes.

## Implementation

### 1. Define trace types (`app/src/shared/contracts.ts`)

```ts
export type SubagentTraceStep = {
  type: "tool_call" | "text";
  toolName?: string;              // present when type === "tool_call"
  argsJson?: string;              // JSON.stringify(toolResult.input) — variable shape per tool
  resultJson?: string;            // JSON.stringify(toolResult.output) — variable shape per tool
  durationMs?: number;            // present when type === "tool_call" (measured via performance.now)
  text?: string;                  // present when type === "text" (PM agent intermediate messages)
};

export type SubagentTrace = {
  agentId: string;        // "pm_agent"
  intent: string;         // "plan_work", "check_status", etc.
  steps: SubagentTraceStep[];
  totalDurationMs: number;
};
```

Add `subagentTraces?: SubagentTrace[]` to `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage`.

### 2. Collect trace in PM agent (`app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`)

Add a `trace` field to `PmAgentOutput`. Wrap `agent.generate()` with `performance.now()` for total duration. After completion, iterate `result.steps` to build the trace:

```ts
const startedAt = performance.now();
const result = await agent.generate({ prompt: ... });
const totalDurationMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);

const traceSteps: SubagentTraceStep[] = [];
for (const step of result.steps) {
  // Capture intermediate text (PM agent reasoning between tool calls)
  if (step.text?.trim()) {
    traceSteps.push({ type: "text", text: step.text.trim() });
  }
  // Capture tool invocations with full args and results
  for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
    traceSteps.push({
      type: "tool_call",
      toolName: toolResult.toolName,
      argsJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.input),
      resultJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.output),
    });
  }
}

const trace: SubagentTrace = {
  agentId: "pm_agent",
  intent: input.intent,
  steps: traceSteps,
  totalDurationMs,
};
```

Note: Per-tool `durationMs` is omitted in v1 since the SDK doesn't expose it. `totalDurationMs` covers the whole agent run. Per-step timing could be added later by wrapping tool `execute` functions.

### 3. Persist trace on message (`app/src/server/chat/chat-route.ts`)

In the `onFinish` callback, extract subagent traces from `responseMessage.parts`. Tool parts for `invoke_pm_agent` have type `"tool-invoke_pm_agent"`:

```ts
// In onFinish:
const subagentTraces: SubagentTrace[] = [];
for (const part of responseMessage.parts) {
  if (part.type === "tool-invoke_pm_agent" && part.state === "output-available") {
    const output = part.output as PmAgentOutput | undefined;
    if (output?.trace) {
      subagentTraces.push(output.trace);
    }
  }
}

await deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
  // ...existing fields...
  ...(subagentTraces.length > 0 ? { subagent_traces: subagentTraces } : {}),
});
```

### 4. Schema migration (`schema/migrations/NNNN_message_subagent_traces.surql`)

Using `TYPE string` for variable-shape args/result to avoid SCHEMAFULL silent key drops:

```surql
BEGIN TRANSACTION;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces ON message TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].agentId ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].intent ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].totalDurationMs ON message TYPE int;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps ON message TYPE array<object>;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].type ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].toolName ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].argsJson ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].resultJson ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].durationMs ON message TYPE option<int>;
DEFINE FIELD OVERWRITE subagent_traces[*].steps[*].text ON message TYPE option<string>;
COMMIT TRANSACTION;
```

Also update `schema/surreal-schema.surql` with the same field definitions.

### 5. Include traces in conversation load (`app/src/server/workspace/workspace-routes.ts`)

Update message SELECT queries to include `subagent_traces`. Map into `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage.subagentTraces`.

Also update `app/src/server/chat/branch-chain.ts` message queries to include the field.

### 6. Reconstruct tool parts on reload (`app/src/client/hooks/use-chat-session.ts`)

Update `bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages` to inject tool parts for messages with traces:

```ts
function bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(messages: WorkspaceBootstrapMessage[]): ChatUIMessage[] {
  return messages.map((m) => {
    const parts: UIMessagePart[] = [{ type: "text" as const, text: m.text }];
    if (m.subagentTraces?.length) {
      for (const trace of m.subagentTraces) {
        parts.push({
          type: "tool-invoke_pm_agent",
          toolCallId: `trace-${m.id}-${trace.agentId}`,
          state: "output-available",
          input: { intent: trace.intent },
          output: { trace },
        } as any);
      }
    }
    return { id: m.id, role: m.role, parts, createdAt: new Date(m.createdAt) };
  });
}
```

### 7. Render collapsible (`app/src/client/routes/chat-page.tsx`)

In the tool-part rendering branch, add handling for `invoke_pm_agent`:

```tsx
if (toolName === "invoke_pm_agent" && toolPart.state === "output-available") {
  const trace = (toolPart.output as any)?.trace as SubagentTrace | undefined;
  if (trace) {
    return (
      <details key={i} className="subagent-trace-block">
        <summary>
          PM Agent — {trace.intent} ({trace.steps.filter(s => s.type === "tool_call").length} tools, {(trace.totalDurationMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s)
        </summary>
        <div className="subagent-trace-steps">
          {trace.steps.map((step, j) => {
            if (step.type === "text") {
              return <div key={j} className="subagent-trace-text">{step.text}</div>;
            }
            return (
              <details key={j} className="subagent-trace-step">
                <summary>
                  <span className="subagent-trace-tool">{step.toolName}</span>
                  {step.durationMs ? <span className="subagent-trace-duration">{step.durationMs}ms</span> : undefined}
                </summary>
                <pre className="subagent-trace-args">{step.argsJson}</pre>
                <pre className="subagent-trace-result">{step.resultJson}</pre>
              </details>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </details>
    );
  }
}
// Fall through to existing generic tool rendering for "Running..." state
```

4. Expand individual tool steps → verify full args/result JSON shown
5. Verify PM agent intermediate text appears between tool steps
6. Reload page → verify collapsible reconstructs from DB with same content
7. Branch conversation → verify traces carry through inherited messages

  "old_string": "  const result = await agent.generate({\n    prompt: [\n      \"You are handling a PM request.\",\n      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,\n      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : \"Project hint: not provided\",\n      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],\n      \"Context:\",\n      input.conversationContext,\n    ].join(\"\\n\"),\n  });\n\n  if (!result.output) {\n    throw new Error(\"pm agent did not produce structured output\");\n  }\n\n  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];\n  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];\n\n  for (const step of result.steps) {\n    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {\n      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n      if (res) {\n        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {\n          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));\n        }\n        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {\n          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ...result.output,\n    extracted_entities,\n    extracted_relationships,\n  };\n}",
  "new_string": "  const startedAt = performance.now();\n\n  const result = await agent.generate({\n    prompt: [\n      \"You are handling a PM request.\",\n      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,\n      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : \"Project hint: not provided\",\n      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],\n      \"Context:\",\n      input.conversationContext,\n    ].join(\"\\n\"),\n  });\n\n  const totalDurationMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);\n\n  if (!result.output) {\n    throw new Error(\"pm agent did not produce structured output\");\n  }\n\n  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];\n  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];\n  const traceSteps: SubagentTraceStep[] = [];\n\n  for (const step of result.steps) {\n    if (step.text?.trim()) {\n      traceSteps.push({ type: \"text\", text: step.text.trim() });\n    }\n\n    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {\n      traceSteps.push({\n        type: \"tool_call\",\n        toolName: toolResult.toolName,\n        argsJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.input),\n        resultJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.output),\n      });\n\n      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n      if (res) {\n        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {\n          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));\n        }\n        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {\n          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ...result.output,\n    extracted_entities,\n    extracted_relationships,\n    trace: {\n      agentId: \"pm_agent\",\n      intent: input.intent,\n      steps: traceSteps,\n      totalDurationMs,\n    },\n  };\n}"
}

   130→
   131→async function queryConversationMessages(
   132→  surreal: Surreal,
   133→  conversationId: string,
   134→  limit: number,
   135→): Promise<MessageRow[]> {
   136→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
   137→  const [rows] = await surreal
   138→    .query<[MessageRow[]]>(
   139→      "SELECT id, role, text, createdAt, suggestions FROM message WHERE conversation = $conversation ORDER BY createdAt ASC LIMIT $limit;",
   140→      { conversation: conversationRecord, limit },
   141→    )
   142→    .collect<[MessageRow[]]>();
   143→  return rows;
   144→}
   145→
   146→/**
   147→ * Load messages from a conversation up to and including the branch point message
   148→ * and its assistant response (the next message after the branch point).
   149→ */
   150→async function queryMessagesUpToBranchPoint(
   151→  surreal: Surreal,
   152→  conversationId: string,
   153→  branchPointCreatedAt: Date,
   154→): Promise<MessageRow[]> {
   155→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
   156→
   157→  // Find the assistant response following the branch point (if any)
   158→  const [nextRows] = await surreal
   159→    .query<[MessageRow[]]>(
   160→      "SELECT id, role, text, createdAt, suggestions FROM message WHERE conversation = $conversation AND createdAt > $branchPointAt ORDER BY createdAt ASC LIMIT 1;",
   161→      { conversation: conversationRecord, branchPointAt: branchPointCreatedAt },
   162→    )
   163→    .collect<[MessageRow[]]>();
   164→
   165→  const cutoffAt = nextRows.length > 0 && nextRows[0].role === "assistant"
   166→    ? (nextRows[0].createdAt instanceof Date ? nextRows[0].createdAt : new Date(nextRows[0].createdAt as string))
   167→    : branchPointCreatedAt;
   168→
   169→  const [rows] = await surreal
   170→    .query<[MessageRow[]]>(
   171→      "SELECT id, role, text, createdAt, suggestions FROM message WHERE conversation = $conversation AND createdAt <= $cutoff ORDER BY createdAt ASC;",
   172→      { conversation: conversationRecord, cutoff: cutoffAt },
   173→    )
   174→    .collect<[MessageRow[]]>();
   175→
   176→  return rows;
   177→}
   178→

   400→async function handleWorkspaceConversation(
   401→  deps: ServerDependencies,
   402→  workspaceId: string,
   403→  conversationId: string,
   404→): Promise<Response> {
   405→  const startedAt = performance.now();
   406→  logInfo("workspace.conversation.started", "Workspace conversation request started", {
   407→    workspaceId,
   408→    conversationId,
   409→  });
   410→
   411→  try {
   412→    const workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, workspaceId);
   413→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
   414→    const conversation = await deps.surreal.select<{
   415→      id: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
   416→      workspace: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
   417→      discusses?: RecordId;
   418→    }>(conversationRecord);
   419→
   420→    if (!conversation) {
   421→      throw new HttpError(404, `conversation not found: ${conversationId}`);
   422→    }
   423→
   424→    if ((conversation.workspace.id as string) !== (workspaceRecord.id as string)) {
   425→      throw new HttpError(400, "conversation does not belong to workspace");
   426→    }
   427→
   428→    const rawMessages = await loadMessagesWithInheritance(deps.surreal, conversationId, 80);
   429→
   430→    const messages = rawMessages.map((row) => ({
   431→      id: row.id,
   432→      role: row.role,
   433→      text: row.text,
   434→      createdAt: toIsoString(row.createdAt),
   435→      ...(row.suggestions && row.suggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: row.suggestions } : {}),
   436→      ...(row.inherited ? { inherited: true } : {}),
   437→    } satisfies WorkspaceBootstrapMessage));
   438→
   439→    let discussEntity: DiscussEntitySummary | undefined;
   440→    if (conversation.discusses) {
   441→      const entityRecord = conversation.discusses as GraphEntityRecord;
   442→      const name = await readEntityName(deps.surreal, entityRecord);
   443→      if (name) {
   444→        const entityRow = await deps.surreal.select<Record<string, unknown>>(entityRecord);
   445→        discussEntity = {
   446→          id: `${entityRecord.table.name}:${entityRecord.id as string}`,
   447→          kind: entityRecord.table.name as EntityKind,
   448→          name,
   449→          ...(entityRow && typeof entityRow.status === "string" ? { status: entityRow.status } : {}),

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/app/src/client/hooks/use-chat-session.ts",
  "old_string": "function bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(messages: WorkspaceBootstrapMessage[]): ChatUIMessage[] {\n  return messages.map((m) => ({\n    id: m.id,\n    role: m.role,\n    parts: [{ type: \"text\" as const, text: m.text }],\n    createdAt: new Date(m.createdAt),\n  }));\n}",
  "new_string": "function bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(messages: WorkspaceBootstrapMessage[]): ChatUIMessage[] {\n  return messages.map((m) => {\n    const parts: ChatUIMessage[\"parts\"] = [{ type: \"text\" as const, text: m.text }];\n    if (m.subagentTraces?.length) {\n      for (const trace of m.subagentTraces) {\n        parts.push({\n          type: \"tool-invoke_pm_agent\",\n          toolCallId: `trace-${m.id}-${trace.agentId}`,\n          state: \"output-available\",\n          input: { intent: trace.intent },\n          output: { trace },\n        } as any);\n      }\n    }\n    return {\n      id: m.id,\n      role: m.role,\n      parts,\n      createdAt: new Date(m.createdAt),\n    };\n  });\n}"
}

> AGENT

All 10 files modified. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**Shared types** (`contracts.ts`): Added `SubagentTraceStep` and `SubagentTrace` types, extended `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage` with optional `subagentTraces`.

**PM Agent** (`agent.ts`): Collects tool steps from `result.steps` — captures intermediate text (`step.text`) and tool calls (`toolResult.toolName`, `.input`, `.output` as JSON strings). Measures total duration via `performance.now()`.

**Chat Route** (`chat-route.ts`): Extracts traces from `responseMessage.parts` in `onFinish` and persists them as `subagent_traces` on the assistant message record.

**Schema** (`0013_message_subagent_traces.surql` + `surreal-schema.surql`): Adds `subagent_traces` field to message table with `argsJson`/`resultJson` as `TYPE string` (avoids SCHEMAFULL silent key drops for variable-shape tool data).

**Conversation load** (`branch-chain.ts` + `workspace-routes.ts`): All message SELECT queries include `subagent_traces`. Mapped through to `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage`.

**Page reload** (`use-chat-session.ts`): `bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages` injects `tool-invoke_pm_agent` parts from stored traces so the collapsible renders on reload.

**UI** (`chat-page.tsx` + `styles.css`): Nested `<details>` collapsible — outer block shows "PM Agent — plan work (3 tools, 2.1s)", inner blocks per tool step with expandable args/result JSON. Intermediate PM agent text shown between steps.

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the smoke test setup and patterns. Let me create a detailed report for you.

## Smoke Test Setup & Patterns - Comprehensive Analysis

### 1. **Test Infrastructure Overview**

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/tests/smoke/`

**17 Smoke Tests** providing integration testing across the full stack:
- `pipeline.test.ts` - Chat agent extraction and message handling
- `onboarding.test.ts` - Workspace bootstrap and onboarding flow
- `conversation-sidebar.test.ts` - Conversation management
- `extraction-quality.test.ts` - Entity extraction validation
- `phase1.test.ts`, `priority.test.ts`, `logging.test.ts`, `authority.test.ts`, and more

**Central Utility:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/tests/smoke/smoke-test-kit.ts`

---

### 2. **Isolated Test Environment Setup** (from `smoke-test-kit.ts`)

#### **Setup Function: `setupSmokeSuite(suiteName: string)`**

Returns a getter function that provides `SmokeTestRuntime`:

```typescript
type SmokeTestRuntime = {
  baseUrl: string;           // HTTP server URL (http://127.0.0.1:PORT)
  surreal: Surreal;          // Connected SurrealDB client
  namespace: string;         // Unique test namespace (smoke_TIMESTAMP_RANDOM)
  database: string;          // Unique test database (SLUG_RANDOM)
  port: number;              // Assigned port for test server
};
```

#### **Setup Sequence (beforeAll hook - 60s timeout):**

1. **Generate isolated IDs:**
   - Namespace: `smoke_{timestamp}_{random}`
   - Database: `{suiteName_slug}_{random}`
   - Port: Random from `smokePortBase` range (default 3100+)

2. **Connect to SurrealDB:**
   - Uses env: `SURREAL_URL` (default `ws://127.0.0.1:8000/rpc`)
   - Authenticate with root credentials
   - Create test namespace (10s timeout)
   - Create test database (10s timeout)

3. **Apply Schema:**
   - Read `schema/surreal-schema.surql`
   - Execute full schema (20s timeout)
   - **Critical:** This is where `subagent_traces` field on message table gets defined

4. **Boot Test Server:**
   - Spawn: `bun run app/server.ts`
   - Pass test-specific env vars:
     - `PORT`, `SURREAL_NAMESPACE`, `SURREAL_DATABASE`
     - `BETTER_AUTH_URL`, `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` (smoke defaults)
     - `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID`, `GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` (smoke defaults)
   - Capture stdout/stderr for debugging

5. **Health Check:**
   - Poll `GET /healthz` until responsive (15s timeout)
   - Check `process.exitCode` — fail immediately if server crashes

#### **Cleanup (afterAll hook - 20s timeout):**

1. Kill server process
2. If setup succeeded and `!SMOKE_KEEP_DB` env:
   - `REMOVE DATABASE ${database}` (10s timeout, best-effort)
   - `REMOVE NAMESPACE ${namespace}` (10s timeout, best-effort)
3. Close SurrealDB connection (2s timeout)

---

### 3. **Test Utilities**

#### **User Creation: `createTestUser(baseUrl, suffix)`**

```typescript
// POST /api/auth/sign-up/email
// Returns: { headers: { Cookie: "..." } }
// Generates unique email: smoke-TIMESTAMP-SUFFIX@test.local
// Password=[REDACTED]
```

**Key:** Returns headers object with session cookie for authenticated requests.

#### **HTTP Helpers:**

- **`fetchJson<T>(url, init?)`** - Fetch + auto-error on non-200
- **`collectSseEvents<T>(streamUrl, timeoutMs)`** - Consumes SSE stream, buffers events, returns array on "done" or timeout

#### **SSE Event Collection Pattern:**

```typescript
// Send message and stream response
const message = await fetchJson<ChatMessageResponse>(`${baseUrl}/api/chat/messages`, {
  method: "POST",
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
  body: JSON.stringify({ clientMessageId, workspaceId, conversationId, text }),
});

// Collect all SSE events until "done" or timeout
const events = await collectSseEvents<StreamEvent>(`${baseUrl}${message.streamUrl}`, 120_000);

// Find specific event types
const assistantEvent = events.find((e) => e.type === "assistant_message");
const extractionEvent = events.find((e) => e.type === "extraction");
```

---

### 4. **Message Persistence Pattern** (from `chat-route.ts`)

#### **User Message Creation (lines 108-113):**

```typescript
await transaction.create(userMessageRecord).content({
  conversation: conversationRecord,
  role: "user",
  text: userText,
  createdAt: now,
});
```

**Key:** Created in transaction, no subagent_traces (those are only on assistant messages).

#### **Assistant Message with Traces (lines 245-253):**

```typescript
// Extract traces from tool-invoke_pm_agent parts
const subagentTraces: SubagentTrace[] = [];
for (const part of responseMessage.parts) {
  if (part.type === "tool-invoke_pm_agent" && part.state === "output-available" && part.output?.trace) {
    subagentTraces.push(part.output.trace as SubagentTrace);
  }
}

// Persist with traces
await deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
  conversation: conversationRecord,
  role: "assistant",
  text: assistantText,
  createdAt: now,
  ...(subagentTraces.length > 0 ? { subagent_traces: subagentTraces } : {}),
});
```

**Key Points:**
- Traces only added if `length > 0`
- Omitted if empty (respects null-free data value contract)
- Created in `onFinish` callback after streaming completes

---

### 5. **Message Loading Pattern** (from `branch-chain.ts`)

#### **Load Messages with Traces (lines 141-147):**

```typescript
async function queryConversationMessages(
  surreal: Surreal,
  conversationId: string,
  limit: number,
): Promise<MessageRow[]> {
  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
  const [rows] = await surreal
    .query<[MessageRow[]]>(
      "SELECT id, role, text, createdAt, suggestions, subagent_traces FROM message WHERE conversation = $conversation ORDER BY createdAt ASC LIMIT $limit;",
      { conversation: conversationRecord, limit },
    )
    .collect<[MessageRow[]]>();
  return rows;
}
```

**Type Definition (lines 21-28):**

```typescript
type MessageRow = {
  id: RecordId<"message", string>;
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  text: string;
  createdAt: Date | string;
  suggestions?: string[];
  subagent_traces?: SubagentTrace[];  // ← Loaded here
};
```

#### **Conversion to API Response (workspace-routes.ts, line 204):**

```typescript
const messages = rawMessages.map((row) => ({
  id: row.id,
  role: row.role,
  text: row.text,
  createdAt: toIsoString(row.createdAt),
  ...(row.suggestions && row.suggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: row.suggestions } : {}),
  ...(row.subagent_traces && row.subagent_traces.length > 0 ? { subagentTraces: row.subagent_traces } : {}),
} satisfies WorkspaceBootstrapMessage));
```

**Pattern:** Traces included in response only if present and non-empty.

---

### 6. **Schema Definition** (from `surreal-schema.surql`)

#### **Message Table (lines 15-35):**

```sql
DEFINE TABLE message SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD conversation ON message TYPE record<conversation>;
DEFINE FIELD role ON message TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ["user", "assistant"];
DEFINE FIELD text ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD createdAt ON message TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD suggestions ON message TYPE option<array<string>>;
DEFINE FIELD clientMessageId ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON message TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces ON message TYPE option<array<object>>;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].agentId ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].intent ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].totalDurationMs ON message TYPE int;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].steps ON message TYPE array<object>;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].steps[*].type ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].steps[*].toolName ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].steps[*].argsJson ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].steps[*].resultJson ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].steps[*].durationMs ON message TYPE option<int>;
DEFINE FIELD subagent_traces[*].steps[*].text ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE INDEX message_conversation_createdAt ON message FIELDS conversation, createdAt;
```

**Key:** `SCHEMAFULL` mode — every nested key in traces must be explicitly declared.

---

### 7. **Database State Verification Patterns**

#### **Pattern 1: Direct Query with RecordId (pipeline.test.ts, lines 78-83):**

```typescript
const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
const [decisionRows] = await surreal
  .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"decision", string>; summary: string }>]>(
    "SELECT id, summary FROM decision WHERE workspace = $workspace;",
    { workspace: workspaceRecord },
  )
  .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"decision", string>; summary: string }>]>();

const hasDecisionInDb = decisionRows.length > 0;
```

**Pattern:**
1. Create `RecordId` object for workspace
2. Use `surreal.query()` with typed result
3. Bind workspace via `$workspace` parameter
4. Call `.collect()` to unwrap results
5. Verify count/properties

#### **Pattern 2: Relationship/Edge Query (pipeline.test.ts, lines 98-103):**

```typescript
const userMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", message.userMessageId);
const [edgeRows] = await surreal
  .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"extraction_relation", string>; evidence?: string }>]>(
    "SELECT id, evidence FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` = $sourceMessage AND out = $decision LIMIT 1;",
    { sourceMessage: userMessageRecord, decision: decision.id },
  )
  .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"extraction_relation", string>; evidence?: string }>]>();

expect(edgeRows.length).toBe(1);
```

#### **Pattern 3: Nested Property Query (extraction-quality.test.ts, lines 106-113):**

```typescript
const [rows] = await surreal
  .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string>; name: string }>]>(
    "SELECT id, name FROM person WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM member_of WHERE out = $workspace);",
    { workspace: workspaceRecord },
  )
  .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string>; name: string }>]>();

return rows;
```

### 8. **Sample Test Flow** (onboarding.test.ts pattern)

```typescript
describe("onboarding smoke", () => {
  it("bootstraps onboarding and completes full onboarding flow", async () => {
    const { baseUrl } = getRuntime();                    // Get test runtime
    const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, "onboarding");  // Create & auth

    // Create workspace
    const create = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string; conversationId: string }>(
      `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Smoke Workspace ${Date.now()}` }),
      },
    );

    expect(create.workspaceId.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // Send chat message
    const firstChat = await fetchJson<ChatMessageResponse>(`${baseUrl}/api/chat/messages`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        clientMessageId: randomUUID(),
        workspaceId: create.workspaceId,
        conversationId: create.conversationId,
        text: "Project: Brain Platform. Decision: use TypeScript first.",
      }),
    });

    // Stream response
    const firstEvents = await collectSseEvents<StreamEvent>(`${baseUrl}${firstChat.streamUrl}`, 180_000);
    expect(firstEvents.some((event) => event.type === "assistant_message")).toBe(true);

    // ... more interactions ...
  }, 600_000);  // 10-minute timeout for full flow
});
```

### 9. **Running Smoke Tests**

```bash
# Run all smoke tests
bun test tests/smoke/

# Run single suite
bun test tests/smoke/pipeline.test.ts

# With custom env
SURREAL_URL=ws://localhost:8000/rpc \
SMOKE_PORT_BASE=3100 \
SMOKE_KEEP_DB=1 \
bun test tests/smoke/

# Keep test DB for inspection after failure
SMOKE_KEEP_DB=1 bun test tests/smoke/onboarding.test.ts
```

**Key Env Variables:**
- `SURREAL_URL` - SurrealDB WebSocket URL
- `SURREAL_USERNAME` / `SURREAL_PASSWORD` - Surreal creds (default: root/root)
- `SMOKE_PORT_BASE` - Base port for server (default: 3100)
- `SMOKE_PORT` - Fixed port (if set, overrides random)
- `SMOKE_KEEP_DB` - Keep test namespace/database after cleanup
- `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` - Auth secret (must be ≥32 chars)
- `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID` / `GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` - OAuth creds

### 10. **Reusable Patterns for Your subagent_traces Test**

#### **Test Structure Template:**

```typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
import { collectSseEvents, createTestUser, fetchJson, setupSmokeSuite } from "./smoke-test-kit";
import type { SubagentTrace } from "../../shared/contracts";

type ChatMessageResponse = {
  messageId: string;
  userMessageId: string;
  conversationId: string;
  workspaceId: string;
  streamUrl: string;
};

const getRuntime = setupSmokeSuite("subagent_traces");

describe("subagent_traces smoke", () => {
  it("persists and loads PM agent traces", async () => {
    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, "traces-1");

    // 1. Create workspace
    const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string; conversationId: string }>(
      `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Traces Smoke ${Date.now()}` }),
      },
    );

    // 2. Complete onboarding
    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    await surreal.update(workspaceRecord).merge({ onboarding_complete: true });

    // 3. Send message that triggers PM agent
    const message = await fetchJson<ChatMessageResponse>(`${baseUrl}/api/chat/messages`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        clientMessageId: randomUUID(),
        workspaceId: workspace.workspaceId,
        conversationId: workspace.conversationId,
        text: "Create a task to implement authentication for Brain Platform",
      }),
    });

    // 4. Collect streaming events
    const events = await collectSseEvents<any>(`${baseUrl}${message.streamUrl}`, 120_000);
    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === "done")).toBe(true);

    // 5. Query database to verify traces persisted
    const assistantMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", message.messageId);
    const [messageRows] = await surreal
      .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"message", string>; subagent_traces?: SubagentTrace[] }>]>(
        "SELECT id, subagent_traces FROM message WHERE id = $messageId;",
        { messageId: assistantMessageRecord },
      )
      .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"message", string>; subagent_traces?: SubagentTrace[] }>]>();

    expect(messageRows.length).toBe(1);
    const assistantMsg = messageRows[0]!;

    // 6. Verify traces structure
    if (assistantMsg.subagent_traces && assistantMsg.subagent_traces.length > 0) {
      const trace = assistantMsg.subagent_traces[0]!;
      expect(typeof trace.agentId).toBe("string");
      expect(typeof trace.intent).toBe("string");
      expect(typeof trace.totalDurationMs).toBe("number");
      expect(Array.isArray(trace.steps)).toBe(true);

      for (const step of trace.steps) {
        expect(["tool_call", "text"]).toContain(step.type);
        if (step.type === "tool_call") {
          expect(typeof step.toolName).toBe("string");
        }
      }
    }

    // 7. Query via bootstrap endpoint to verify traces in response
    const bootstrap = await fetchJson<any>(
      `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(workspace.workspaceId)}/bootstrap`,
    );

    const msgWithTraces = bootstrap.messages.find(
      (m: any) => m.id === message.messageId && m.subagentTraces,
    );
    expect(msgWithTraces).toBeDefined();
    if (msgWithTraces) {
      expect(Array.isArray(msgWithTraces.subagentTraces)).toBe(true);
    }
  }, 180_000);
});
```

---

### 11. **Key Takeaways for Your Testing Needs**

1. **Schema is Applied During Setup**
   - The `subagent_traces` field on `message` table is defined in `schema/surreal-schema.surql`
   - Applied automatically when each smoke test runs `beforeAll`
   - No separate migration step needed in tests

2. **Message Persistence Already Works**
   - `chat-route.ts` line 247-253 shows traces are persisted in `onFinish` callback
   - Omitted if empty (respects null-free contract)

3. **Message Loading Already Includes Traces**
   - `branch-chain.ts` line 143 explicitly selects `subagent_traces`
   - `workspace-routes.ts` line 204 includes in `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage` response if present

4. **To Test Traces Round-Trip:**
   - Create workspace and complete onboarding
   - Send message triggering PM agent with work item creation intent
   - Stream SSE until `done`
   - Query `message` table directly to verify `subagent_traces` field has expected structure
   - Load via `/api/workspaces/:id/bootstrap` to verify traces in API response
   - Verify `WorkspaceBootstrapMessage` contract matches (camelCase: `subagentTraces`)

5. **RecordId Pattern is Standard**
   - All tests use `new RecordId("table", id)` for type-safe DB access
   - Bind parameters with `$name` syntax
   - Result `.collect()` unwraps query results

6. **Assertion Patterns**
   - Count existence: `expect(rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)`
   - Type check: `expect(typeof value).toBe("string")`
   - Array check: `expect(Array.isArray(value)).toBe(true)`
   - Enum values: `expect(["a", "b"]).toContain(value)`

    16→const surrealPassword = process.env.SURREAL_PASSWORD ?? "root";
    17→const smokePortBase = Number(process.env.SMOKE_PORT_BASE ?? "3100");
    18→
    19→export function setupSmokeSuite(suiteName: string): () => SmokeTestRuntime {
    20→  const runId = `${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`;
    21→  const namespace = `smoke_${runId}`;
    22→  const suiteSlug = suiteName
    23→    .toLowerCase()
    24→    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
    25→    .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
    26→  const database = `${suiteSlug || "suite"}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`;
    27→
    28→  let runtime: SmokeTestRuntime | undefined;
    29→  let serverProcess: ReturnType<typeof Bun.spawn> | undefined;
    30→  let setupSucceeded = false;
    31→
    32→  beforeAll(async () => {
    33→    const port = process.env.SMOKE_PORT
    34→      ? Number(process.env.SMOKE_PORT)
    35→      : smokePortBase + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20000);
    36→    if (!Number.isFinite(port) || port <= 0) {
    37→      throw new Error(`Invalid smoke test port value: ${port}`);
    38→    }
    39→    const baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
    40→
    41→    const surreal = new Surreal();
    42→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.connect(surrealUrl), 10_000, "connect to SurrealDB");
    43→    await withTimeout(
    44→      () => surreal.signin({ username: surrealUsername, password: surrealPassword }),
    45→      10_000,
    46→      "authenticate with SurrealDB",
    47→    );
    48→
    49→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.query(`DEFINE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`), 10_000, "define test namespace");
    50→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.use({ namespace }), 10_000, "switch to test namespace");

   221→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>();
   222→
   223→  return taskRows.length + decisionRows.length + questionRows.length + projectRows.length;

   121→export async function createTestUser(baseUrl: string, suffix: string): Promise<TestUser> {
   122→  const email = `smoke-${Date.now()}-${suffix}@test.local`;
   123→  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
   124→    method: "POST",
   125→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   126→    body: JSON.stringify({ name: "Smoke Tester", email, password=[REDACTED]" }),
   127→    redirect: "manual",
   128→  });
   129→
   130→  if (!response.ok) {
   131→    const body = await response.text();
   132→    throw new Error(`Failed to create test user (${response.status}): ${body}`);
   133→  }
   134→
   135→  const setCookie = response.headers.getSetCookie();
   136→  if (!setCookie || setCookie.length === 0) {
   137→    throw new Error("Sign-up did not return session cookies");
   138→  }
   139→
   140→  const cookieHeader = setCookie.map((c) => c.split(";")[0]).join("; ");
   141→  return { headers: { Cookie: cookieHeader } };
   142→}
   143→
   144→export async function collectSseEvents<T extends { type: string }>(streamUrl: string, timeoutMs: number): Promise<T[]> {
   145→  const response = await fetch(streamUrl, { headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" } });
   146→  if (!response.ok || !response.body) {
   147→    throw new Error(`Failed to open SSE stream (${response.status})`);
   148→  }
   149→
   150→  const reader = response.body.getReader();

   414→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
   415→    const conversation = await deps.surreal.select<{
   416→      id: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
   417→      workspace: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
   418→      discusses?: RecordId;
   419→    }>(conversationRecord);
   420→
   421→    if (!conversation) {
   422→      throw new HttpError(404, `conversation not found: ${conversationId}`);
   423→    }
   424→
   425→    if ((conversation.workspace.id as string) !== (workspaceRecord.id as string)) {
   426→      throw new HttpError(400, "conversation does not belong to workspace");
   427→    }
   428→
   429→    const rawMessages = await loadMessagesWithInheritance(deps.surreal, conversationId, 80);
   430→
   431→    const messages = rawMessages.map((row) => ({
   432→      id: row.id,
   433→      role: row.role,
   434→      text: row.text,
   435→      createdAt: toIsoString(row.createdAt),
   436→      ...(row.suggestions && row.suggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: row.suggestions } : {}),
   437→      ...(row.inherited ? { inherited: true } : {}),
   438→      ...(row.subagent_traces && row.subagent_traces.length > 0 ? { subagentTraces: row.subagent_traces } : {}),
   439→    } satisfies WorkspaceBootstrapMessage));
   440→
   441→    let discussEntity: DiscussEntitySummary | undefined;
   442→    if (conversation.discusses) {
   443→      const entityRecord = conversation.discusses as GraphEntityRecord;
   444→      const name = await readEntityName(deps.surreal, entityRecord);
   445→      if (name) {
   446→        const entityRow = await deps.surreal.select<Record<string, unknown>>(entityRecord);
   447→        discussEntity = {
   448→          id: `${entityRecord.table.name}:${entityRecord.id as string}`,
   449→          kind: entityRecord.table.name as EntityKind,
   450→          name,
   451→          ...(entityRow && typeof entityRow.status === "string" ? { status: entityRow.status } : {}),
   452→        };
   453→      }
   454→    }
   455→
   456→    const payload: WorkspaceConversationResponse = {
   457→      conversationId,
   458→      messages,
   459→      ...(discussEntity ? { discussEntity } : {}),
   460→    };
   461→
   462→    logInfo("workspace.conversation.completed", "Workspace conversation request completed", {
   463→      workspaceId,
   464→      conversationId,
   465→      messageCount: messages.length,
   466→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   467→    });
   468→
   469→    return jsonResponse(payload, 200);
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/zurich-v2/app/src/server/agents/AGENTS.md",
  "content": "## Subagent Execution Trace Contract\n\nEvery subagent MUST return a `SubagentTrace` in its output so the chat agent can persist it and the frontend can render a collapsible execution log.\n\n### What to capture\n\n| Step type | Fields | Source |\n|-----------|--------|--------|\n| `tool_call` | `toolName`, `argsJson` (stringified input), `resultJson` (stringified output) | `step.toolResults[i]` |\n| `text` | `text` (intermediate reasoning between tool calls) | `step.text` |\n\nAlso measure `totalDurationMs` by wrapping the `agent.generate()` call with `performance.now()`.\n\n### How to implement\n\n1. Import the shared types:\n   ```ts\n   import type { SubagentTrace, SubagentTraceStep } from \"../../../shared/contracts\";\n   ```\n\n2. Collect steps after `agent.generate()`:\n   ```ts\n   const startedAt = performance.now();\n   const result = await agent.generate({ prompt: ... });\n   const totalDurationMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);\n\n   const traceSteps: SubagentTraceStep[] = [];\n   for (const step of result.steps) {\n     if (step.text?.trim()) {\n       traceSteps.push({ type: \"text\", text: step.text.trim() });\n     }\n     for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {\n       traceSteps.push({\n         type: \"tool_call\",\n         toolName: toolResult.toolName,\n         argsJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.input),\n         resultJson: JSON.stringify(toolResult.output),\n       });\n     }\n   }\n   ```\n\n3. Include the trace in the agent output type:\n   ```ts\n   return {\n     ...result.output,\n     trace: {\n       agentId: \"your_agent_id\",\n       intent: input.intent,\n       steps: traceSteps,\n       totalDurationMs,\n     },\n   };\n   ```\n\n### Persistence path\n\nThe chat agent's `onFinish` callback in `chat-route.ts` extracts traces from tool parts matching `tool-invoke_<agent>` and persists them as `subagent_traces` on the assistant message record. The conversation load endpoints include traces in the API response for page reload reconstruction.\n\n### Schema constraints\n\n- `argsJson` and `resultJson` are stored as `TYPE string` (JSON-stringified) because SurrealDB SCHEMAFULL silently drops undeclared nested keys from `TYPE object`.\n- If adding a new subagent, add its tool name to the trace extraction loop in `chat-route.ts`:\n  ```ts\n  if (part.type === \"tool-invoke_pm_agent\" && ...) // ← add your agent's tool here\n  ```\n\n### Frontend rendering\n\nThe frontend renders traces as a collapsible `<details>` block in `chat-page.tsx`. The `toolName` in the part type (e.g., `tool-invoke_pm_agent`) determines which traces get rendered. Add rendering for new subagent tool names in the `isToolPart` branch.\n"
}
```
